### Finish sheet  (web: `components/book/FinishSheet.vue`, `components/ui/RatingInput.vue`, `stores/reading.ts`)
Purpose: finish the open read with an end day, an optional quarter-star Rating and an optional review (D's finish-sheet; issue #1, stories 32–34).
Layout: `UiSheet` (Cancel, title `finish.title`), the Book, one grouped row `finish.endedOn` (as in the Start sheet), the rating control, under it the Read as segments (`finish.readAs`, online only; see *Read as*), the review box (`finish.review` · `finish.optional`, placeholder `finish.reviewPlaceholder`, serif italic, a lamp ring while focused), the error if any, the primary **Finish**.
Rating control: the eyebrow `rating.title` with `rating.optional` (or **Clear**, `rating.clear`, once rated) opposite; the value large in the accent with `rating.outOf` ("3.75 / 5"), or `rating.none` until there is one; five 44-pt stars filled to the quarter; under them a rail with 21 notches (0–5 in quarters, whole stars taller, the ones up to the value lit) and a thumb at the value, with a thin guide up into the stars; the hint `rating.hint`.
States: choosing (end day today, no Rating, empty review) · busy (`finish.busy`, button disabled, the control and the review locked) · error (`library.error.<code>` above the button; the button reads `finish.retry`)
Actions → result:
- Drag across the stars → the Rating follows the finger and snaps to the nearest quarter notch (`utils/rating.ts`, `quartersAt`); dragging off the left of the first star empties it. Vertical drags still scroll the sheet, and the sheet never takes a drag on the control for a swipe down.
- Tap a star → that many whole stars (`wholeStarsAt`). Clear → no Rating.
- Keyboard / assistive tech: the control is a slider (0–5, `rating.label` as its value text): arrows ±¼, Page Up/Down ±1, Home empties, End is 5.
- Finish (`finish.submit`) → the day is checked on the device (missing, after today, before the read's start → `ended_before_started`); then **one database call**, `finish_reading(p_entry_id, p_ended_on, p_rating, p_review)`: the open session closes as finished with the day, the Rating in quarters (1–20, 3.75 = 15) and the review trimmed (blank is none). The entry's Status turns to Finished in the database; the sheet closes; the Book moves from Currently reading to Finished in the Library and the book page shows its Rating.
- Cancel, the scrim, a swipe down, Escape → closes; what was chosen stays for the next time the same Book's sheet opens (until it is finished or another member signs in).
Edge cases: the review takes at most 10,000 characters (`review_too_long` from the database otherwise). On a short screen the sheet scrolls; a downward drag on its scrolled content scrolls it back and only dismisses once it is at the top (any `UiSheet`). Reading the same Book's sheet on two devices: the second finish gets `not_reading`.
Copy keys: `finish.title`, `finish.endedOn`, `finish.review`, `finish.optional`, `finish.reviewPlaceholder`, `finish.action`, `finish.busy`, `finish.retry`, `rating.title`, `rating.optional`, `rating.clear`, `rating.outOf`, `rating.none`, `rating.label`, `rating.hint`, `common.today|yesterday|dayMonth|dayMonthYear|chooseDay`, `library.error.*`, `common.cancel`
IDs: `finish`, `finish.cancel`, `finish.scrim`, `finish.date`, `finish.rating` (the slider), `finish.rating.value`, `finish.rating.clear`, `finish.review`, `finish.error`, `finish.submit`
Flow: `web/e2e/start-and-finish.spec.ts` (a tap gives 2 stars, a drag 3.75, a review, Finish → Finished; the stored session has 15 quarters); the control's geometry in `web/tests/rating-control-and-days.test.ts`, the repository in `web/tests/reading.test.ts`, the rules in `supabase/tests/reading_sessions_test.sql`
Haptics (web: `utils/haptics.ts`, issue #64): one short tick each time the Rating moves to another step (a quarter while dragging, a whole star on a tap, a key press; not when it stays put): `navigator.vibrate(8)` where the browser has it (Android Chrome); on iOS Safari, which has no Vibration API, a hidden `<label><input type=checkbox switch></label>` is clicked inside the gesture (Safari 17.4+); nowhere else (macOS trackpad haptics are not exposed to the web). Reduce Motion does not silence it, a haptic is not motion; system settings do.
Native: a sheet; the rating control is a custom control with the same geometry (`ratingX`), a system haptic on each quarter (selection feedback), following the system's haptics setting.

### DNF sheet  (web: `components/book/AbandonSheet.vue`, `stores/reading.ts`)
Naming (issue #64): the member sees **DNF** (did not finish) everywhere: `status.notFinished`, `library.view.status.notFinished`, `library.notFinishedOn`, `book.abandon` (the button in the Update progress sheet), the sheet's title and action ("Mark as DNF"). Copy only: the session outcome stays `abandoned`, keys and test IDs keep their names.
Purpose: stop the open read without pretending it was finished: an end day and an optional reason (issue #10; issue #1, story 35). The Book moves to Finished and shows under *DNF*.
Opened from: **DNF** (`progress.abandon`) in the Update progress sheet, under Finish (the book page has no DNF of its own). Like Finish there, it saves the wheel first (the read keeps where she put it down; nothing is sent when the wheel was not moved; a refused save stays in the progress sheet with its reason), then this sheet takes over. What follows is unchanged: the same sheet, the same day check and the same one call; there is no undo.
Layout: `UiSheet` (Cancel, title `abandon.title`), the Book, one grouped row `abandon.endedOn` (as in the Start and Finish sheets), the reason box (`abandon.reason` · `abandon.optional`, placeholder `abandon.reasonPlaceholder`, the same serif italic box as the review), the error if any, the primary **Abandon** (slash icon).
States: choosing (day today, empty reason) · busy (`abandon.busy`, button disabled, the reason locked) · error (`library.error.<code>` above the button, the day error-coloured for a day problem; the button reads `abandon.retry`)
Actions → result:
- The day row → the platform's own date picker; latest day today, earliest the day the read started.
- Mark as DNF (`abandon.submit`) → the day is checked on the device (missing, after today, before the read's start → `ended_before_started`); then **one database call**, `abandon_reading(p_entry_id, p_ended_on, p_reason)`: the open session closes as abandoned with the day and the reason trimmed (blank is none, at most 1,000 characters). The entry's Status turns to Finished in the database; the sheet closes; the Book leaves Currently reading and appears in Finished and under *DNF*, and the book page shows DNF with Start again.
- Cancel, the scrim, a swipe down, Escape → closes; the typed reason and day stay for the next time the same Book's sheet opens (until it is abandoned or another member signs in).
Edge cases: a second device ending the read first → `not_reading`; a reason over 1,000 characters → `reason_too_long` (the box stops at the limit). An abandoned session has no Rating and no review.

IDs: `abandon`, `abandon.cancel`, `abandon.scrim`, `abandon.date`, `abandon.reason`, `abandon.error`, `abandon.submit`
Flow: no flow (`web/tests/abandon.test.ts` holds it); the rules in `supabase/tests/abandon_and_read_again_test.sql`, the repository in `web/tests/abandon.test.ts`

### Library  (web: `app/pages/library.vue`, `components/library/{EntryRow,ReadingCard,ViewBar,FilterSheet,SortSheet}.vue`, `components/ui/Pill.vue`, `stores/libraryView.ts`, `data/libraryView.ts`)
Purpose: the member's Books by Status.
Layout: the tab header (Library), the Status segments with their counts (`library.segment.*`; the chosen one lit with a lamp hairline), then the list's **view bar** (see *Library filters and sort*), then the chosen segment's entries. *Finished* has no row of pills of its own: giving a read up (*Not finished*, DNF) is a filter of the view bar.
States: loading (first visit, nothing yet) · empty Library (the lamp over the empty shelf: `library.emptyTitle`, `library.empty`, the search prompt) · Want to read (rows: cover, serif title, authors, `library.added` with the date in mono; newest added first) · Currently reading (a card per Book on the raised surface in its cover's light: cover with its glow, serif title, authors, `book.since` "Since 3 Oct · day 4", and a quiet **Finish** on the card; newest start first) · Finished (grouped by the year of the end date, an eyebrow with the year and its count; rows: cover, title, authors, the Rating as small stars with its value or `rating.none`, then the end day; newest end first, entries without an end date last under `library.undated`; an abandoned read is dimmed (cover faded and grey, title and authors quiet) and says a slash icon and `library.notFinishedOn` "DNF · 3 Oct" instead of a Rating) · Finished filtered to *Not finished* (the same rows and year groups, only the entries whose latest session was abandoned; none → `library.viewEmpty`) · a segment without entries (`library.segmentEmpty.<status>.title|text`) · load error (`library.loadError`, Retry)
Actions → result:
- Tap a segment → shows its entries. The counts are each list's length. The segments are tabs (WAI-ARIA tabs): one Tab stop (the chosen one), ←/→ and Home/End move to a segment and show it, the entries under them are its tab panel. A Finished year's heading is named "2026: 15 books" (`library.yearHeading`), not the year and its count run together.
- Finish on a Currently reading card (`library.finish`) → the Finish sheet over the Library; after it the Book leaves Currently reading and appears in Finished in its place.
- Tap a row → the book page `/book/<id>`, started on touch-down (`UiPressLink`).
Edge cases: the page is kept alive: coming back (from a book page, another tab) finds the same segment and scroll position, and the list refreshes in the background **unless it is fresh**: the three lists are not asked for again within 60 s of the last answer (`FRESH_MS` in `utils/fresh.ts`, `load({ ifStale })` in `stores/library.ts`; a change made on this device is in the lists already, one made on another device shows at the first visit after the window; coming back online, a drained outbox and an import ask at once; the Playwright flows' build has the window off, they play the other device). An add, start or finish moves the entry into its list, in its place (`library.entryChanged`, the same order the database sorts by: `sortEntries`) — on screen only once the sheet that did it has left (`useSettled`) or, for a change made on another screen, once the Library shows again; then the row or card leaving closes its room and the ones after it slide up, a new one opens its room and fades in (`UiListMotion`, docs/MOTION.md). Finished's year headers stay pinned under the status bar while their year scrolls by. Rows take the pressed fill on press (and, with a mouse, a lighter one on hover). The three lists load together, each as one request with the entries' latest sessions (`latest_session`). **The lists carry no description** (`LIST_BOOK_COLUMNS` in `data/library.ts`: every `books` column but `description` and `owner_id`, about 65 % of a Book's JSON): the book page reads it from the device's descriptions (see Offline and install) or from its own request. *Not finished* is a filter of Finished, not a Status (the Filter sheet's **Status** section, offered while something was given up): `isNotFinished(entry)` (Finished and its latest session abandoned; a read abandoned and later finished or started again is not) over the Finished list, the same question as `latest_session(e).outcome = 'abandoned'` in the database. Signing out (or another member signing in) forgets the list.

### Library filters and sort  (web: `components/library/{ViewBar,FilterSheet,SortSheet}.vue` and `components/ui/Pill.vue` in `app/pages/library.vue`, `stores/libraryView.ts`, `data/libraryView.ts` (filters, sorts, what the device remembers); no database call)
Purpose: find a book in a big Library: filter and sort each Status list (#169, shared decisions in #166).
Layout: under the Status segments, quiet and one row high: the **Filter** pill (`library.view.filter`, lit with the number of filters set once any is) and the **Sort** pill (`library.view.sortBy`, "Sort: Date read"; lit when it is not the list's own order), the result count at the right (`library.view.count` "30 books"; `library.view.countOf` "12 of 30 books" while a filter is set), a polite status. While filters are set a second row: each as a removable chip (`library.view.chip`, its × named `library.view.removeChip`) and **Clear** (`library.view.clear`). One row high on every segment; the segments' counts stay the lists' own.
Facets (each Status list has its own filters, kept apart): **Status** (Finished only: Finished, or Not finished = the latest read was given up; offered while something was given up; it replaces the old All / DNF pills), **Read as** (Physical, Ebook, Audiobook, Not set; offered once any book has one), **Author** (any author of the book; a search field once more than 12), **Rating** (5 stars, 4 and up, 3 and up, Not rated), **Year read** (the year the latest read ended in, "No end date"), **Pages** (From / To, the page count that counts: her own total before the edition's; a book without one never passes a range). Rating and Year read only under Finished. **Genre** (#168) is a pluggable facet: `stores/libraryView.ts` `provideGenres(lookup)` is the seam; `stores/genres.ts` provides it as soon as it holds her Library's genres (the device keeps them, so it is there at the first frame and offline); until then there is no section and no chip. The pills are the genres her books have (hers where she corrected them, else the computed ones), most books first with their count, any-of; the labels are `genre.<id>`. Filters combine: every facet set must pass, several values of one facet are any-of.
Sort: Want to read: date added, title, author, pages; Currently reading adds the date started; Finished: date read, date added, rating, title, author, pages. A choice goes its natural way (newest, highest, longest, A to Z); the chosen row again turns it round (`library.view.sortDir.*`). A book without the value (no rating, no end date, no page count, no author) is last either way; ties keep the list's own order. Author sorts by the last word of the first author's name. Finished is grouped by year only while sorted by date read; another sort is one list.
States: nothing set (the list's own order, `data/libraryView.ts` returns the loaded list itself) · filters set (chips, count) · nothing matches (`library.viewEmpty`: `library.view.empty.*` and **Clear filters**, the view bar stays) · no entries in the list (no view bar).
Actions → result:
- Filter (`library.view.filter`) → the sheet (`libraryFilter`, Cancel · Filter · **Apply**): pills for each facet (`libraryFilter.readAs.<way>`, `libraryFilter.author`, `libraryFilter.rating.<stars|unrated>`, `libraryFilter.year`, `libraryFilter.pagesMin|pagesMax`), the count it would leave live at the top (`libraryFilter.count`) and **Clear all**. It works on a copy: Apply makes it the list's view (the list changes once the sheet has left, `useSettled`), Cancel leaves it.
- Sort (`library.view.sort`) → the sheet (`librarySort`, rows `librarySort.<key>`, the chosen one says its direction `librarySort.<key>.dir`); a tap chooses and closes.
- A chip's × (`library.view.chip`) takes that filter off at once; Clear (`library.view.clear`) all of them, the sort stays.
- The last choice is remembered per member on this device (`libellus-library-view`, a map of member id → the three lists' views; outside the `libellus.` prefix, so signing out keeps it, like the import hint) and read back before the first frame: a Library that opens filtered is filtered from its first paint, nothing shifts. Unreadable or unknown values are the default.
Edge cases: Not finished is the Status facet of Finished and combines with the others. A filter whose value no book has any more stays visible as a chip (and in the sheet) so it can be taken off. Chips scroll sideways rather than wrap. Keyboard: the pills are toggle buttons (`aria-pressed`), the count is a polite status, the sheets are modal dialogs.
Copy keys: `library.view.*` (`label`, `filter`, `sort`, `sortBy`, `count`, `countOf`, `clear`, `clearAll`, `chipsLabel`, `removeChip`, `apply`, `showCount`, `sheetTitle`, `sortTitle`, `empty.*`, `facet.*`, `readAs.*`, `rating.*`, `pages.*`, `author.*`, `sortKey.*`, `sortDir.*`, and the labels `genre.<id>`), `library.undated`, `common.cancel`
IDs: `library.view`, `library.view.filter`, `library.view.sort`, `library.view.count`, `library.view.chips`, `library.view.chip`, `library.view.clear`, `library.viewEmpty`, `library.viewEmpty.clear`, `libraryFilter` (`.cancel`, `.action`, `.count`, `.clear`, `.section.<facet>`, `.status.<finished|notFinished>`, `.readAs.<physical|ebook|audiobook|unset>`, `.author`, `.authorSearch`, `.rating.<5|4|3|unrated>`, `.year`, `.pagesMin`, `.pagesMax`, `.genre`), `librarySort` (`.<dateRead|dateAdded|rating|title|author|pages>`, `.<key>.dir`)
Flow: no flow for the filters and the sort (`web/tests/library-view.test.ts`) and none for the layout shift; the rules in `web/tests/library-view.test.ts`
Native: the same two pills, chips and sheets; the choice kept in the app's preferences per member.

### Read as  (web: `components/book/ReadAs.vue` in `components/book/OptionsSheet.vue` and `components/book/FinishSheet.vue`, `setReadAs` in `data/library.ts` and `stores/library.ts`, `finishReadAs` in `stores/reading.ts`, `data/readAs.ts`; database: `library_entries.read_as`, `set_read_as`)
Purpose: how the member read this Book: on paper, as an ebook or as an audiobook. Hers, not the edition's (#166, #169): an edition does not say how she read it, so a word she never said stays unsaid (the edition's format is only the default shown). Changing edition presets her word when she had one and the new edition is read another way (see Edge cases). The Library's Read as filter uses it.
Layout: the eyebrow `readAs.label` over one row of three icon segments (`readAs.physical|ebook|audiobook`: a book, a reader, headphones), the same control as the edition's format row (`UiIconSegments`: the reader's Margins / Line spacing style, the chosen segment on the raised surface unfolding its name beside the icon, the others icons only; a radio group, arrow keys move the choice), at the top of the Book's options sheet (entries only) and under the rating in the Finish sheet. Not on the Book page itself (owner: the page carries one action; the options sheet is enough).
States: not said, and the edition's format unknown (no segment lit) · hers (that segment lit) · not said, the edition's format known (its segment lit as the default: hardcover, paperback → Physical; ebook; audiobook, `readAsOf`; the formats are PR #165's, nothing is lit by them before it) · offline (the segments disabled and `readAs.offline`; in the Finish sheet the field is left out, the finish itself works offline) · refused (`library.error.read_as_invalid`)
Actions → result:
- Tap a segment → that way is hers: **one database call**, `set_read_as(p_entry_id, p_read_as)`; the options sheet and the Library's rows follow at once. Tap hers again → null, not said (the default lights again). Tapping the lit default makes it hers.
- In the Finish sheet the choice starts from what she said, nothing is saved until Finish (`finish.submit`); a changed one is then sent with `set_read_as` (not in the outbox: it needs the connection, the finish does not).
Edge cases: `read_as` belongs to the entry, not to a read: Finish and Read again leave it. **Change edition (and My edition isn't listed) presets it from the new edition's format**, in the same transaction as the move (`move_entry_to`; `read_as_for_format`): the format that counts for the new edition (the one she said for it in the sheet, else its Book's) read as paper (hardcover, paperback), ebook or audiobook replaces her word, but only where her word was set and the new edition is read another way than the one the entry leaves (paper → ebook, an unknown format → any); a word she never said stays unsaid (its default follows the new edition's format by itself), an edition of unknown format leaves her word alone, and a move between editions read the same way (paper → paper) keeps her word, so "audiobook" said of a paperback survives another paperback. She can set it again in the segments (`book.readAs`). Saving only her format for the edition she has (**Save**) never touches it. Only her own entry (`entry_not_found` for any other), only the three words (`read_as_invalid`; the table check backs it). The answer's Read as is laid over the device's copy of the entry, so a finish still waiting in the outbox is not undone by it.
Copy keys: `readAs.label`, `readAs.physical|ebook|audiobook`, `readAs.offline`, `library.error.read_as_invalid`
IDs: `bookOptions.readAs` (`.physical|ebook|audiobook`, `.offline`, `.error`), `finish.readAs.*`
Flow: no flow; the filter in `web/tests/library-view.test.ts`, the repository in `web/tests/read-as.test.ts`, the rules in `supabase/tests/read_as_test.sql`
Native: three toggle buttons (a segmented control that may be empty).

### Genres  (web: `components/book/Genres.vue` under the facts line in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`, `components/book/GenreSheet.vue`, `components/profile/Genres.vue` in `app/pages/profile/{index,[year]}.vue`, the Genre facet of `components/library/FilterSheet.vue`, `stores/genres.ts`, `showGenre` and `focus` in `stores/libraryView.ts`, `data/enrich/{bookGenres,genres,genreFigures,deviceGenres}.ts`; database: `book_genres`, `library_genres`, `set_entry_genres`, `reset_entry_genres`, see `docs/ENRICHMENT.md`)
Purpose: up to three canonical genres a Book (#168): shown as chips, set and corrected by the member for her own entry, filtered by in the Library and counted on the Profile. The twenty ids are stable (`data/enrich/genres.ts`), their labels are `genre.<id>`; no source is ever named.
Layout: on the Book page the chips sit in a wrapping row under the facts line (not in the title block), then the quiet word **Edit** (or **Add genres** when there are none). A Book in her Library always has the row, at the height of a chip, so nothing moves when the chips arrive (they are on the device already: `libellus.genres`, written by `stores/genres.ts` after every load and correction); while a Book of hers is still unknown a placeholder chip stands in. A Book outside her Library shows the chips only when it has genres (opening its room, `UiReveal`) and has no Edit. The sheet (`genreSheet`, Cancel · Genres · **Save**) lists the twenty as pills in canonical order, up to three; with three chosen the rest are disabled; a polite count "2 of 3 chosen"; **Back to suggested** underneath, always in the layout, inert while the choice is not hers. The Profile's **Genres** block (after the ratings, in a year in review its top three) is one row per genre: the name, a bar on a hairline filling to its share, the share in mono; the header says "Of N books" and "· M without a genre".
States: chips (hers or computed) · none (Add genres) · not yet known (placeholder chip; nothing offline and never loaded) · sheet: saving · failed (`genreSheet.error`, Save is "Try again") · offline (the action disabled and says Offline; the Back to suggested too) · Profile: loading placeholders in the wave, empty (the block closes), a row per genre.
Actions → result:
- Edit / Add genres → the sheet, starting from the genres the Book has now; tap a pill → on or off, in her order; **Save** → one call `set_entry_genres(p_entry, p_genres)` (0–3 ids, duplicates folded); the chips, the Library filter and the Profile follow at once.
- Back to suggested → `reset_entry_genres(p_entry)`, the computed genres return.
- A Profile row → the Library, **Finished**, with every other filter off, that genre and (a year in view, or a year in review) that year set (`libraryView.showGenre`); the Library opens on it (`libraryView.focus`).
Edge cases: the figures count a finished read in the year it ended, re-reads included, a Book once in each of its genres, so shares may add up to more than 100 %; the share is of the reads that have a genre (`genreFiguresOf`, `web/tests/genre-figures.test.ts`). Her correction is per entry (it goes with Change edition, not with another member). Writes need the connection: they are not in the outbox.
Copy keys: `genre.<id>` (twenty labels), `genre.book.*`, `genre.sheet.*`, `genre.stats.*`, `common.offline`, `common.cancel`
IDs: `book.genres`, `book.genre` (each chip, `data-genre`), `book.genresEdit`, `genreSheet` (`.cancel`, `.action`, `.count`, `.genre.<id>`, `.reset`, `.error`), `libraryFilter.section.genre`, `libraryFilter.genre`, `profile.genres`, `profile.genresPlaced`, `profile.genre.<id>`
Flow: no flow (`web/tests/enrich.test.ts` for the genres of a Book and her correction, `web/tests/library-view.test.ts` for the filter), the aggregation in `web/tests/genre-figures.test.ts`
Native: chips in a flow layout; a sheet of toggle chips with a counter; bars as a list.

### Home  (web: `app/pages/index.vue`, `components/home/{Empty,ImportOffer,NextInSeries,NextRow,NextSheet,ReadingCard,Tally,TallySheet,UpNext}.vue`, `composables/useGreeting.ts`)
Purpose: what matters now: the Books being read, how many were finished this year, what is Want to read (D's home / home-empty; issue #1, stories on Home).
Layout: the tab header (date eyebrow, greeting — "Good evening, Fabian" when she gave a name, see Member name — avatar), then *Currently reading* (eyebrow `home.reading` with the count opposite; no count while nothing is being read), the year's tally, *Want to read*, then, when there is one, *Next in your series* (see that entry: the series she has started, five at a time, a See more sheet for the rest). Last, *Your circle* (see that entry), hidden with no request, card or row.
- **Currently reading** card per open read, newest start first (the Library's order): on the raised surface lit by its cover's light, the cover large (82 pt) with its glow, serif title, authors, `book.since` ("Since 3 Oct · day 4": the start day is day 1), the progress bar and value with a quiet **Update** at the bottom (and, at the last page, "The end." with a lit **Finish**: see *Reading progress*). Nothing being read: the line `home.readingEmpty`.
- **Tally**, between hairlines: `home.readIn` ("Read in 2026") over tally marks, one thin lamp stroke per finished read, a gap after every fifth (wrapping onto further lines), and the count large at the right. The count is **finished sessions whose end date falls in the current calendar year, re-reads included** (each finished read counts, however often the same Book was read; abandoned and open reads and finished reads without an end date do not). The year is the member's own today (`isoDay`), not the server's. Accessible name `home.readInLabel`; with at least one Book read this year the strip is **one button** (press feedback: `fillStrong` behind it, a ghost chevron after the count, `aria-haspopup="dialog"`) that opens the year's Books (*Read in sheet*); with none it stays a plain strip, since there would be nothing to open.
- **Want to read**: newest added first, at most 8 covers (72 pt) in a row that scrolls sideways past the screen's edge, with **See all N** (`home.seeAll`) to the Library. Hidden when there is nothing on Want to read.
- **Read in sheet** (`HomeTallySheet`, the Profile's reads sheet `ProfileReadsSheet` with its own IDs `homeTally.*`), titled `home.readIn` ("Read in 2026"). **The shelf owner's sheet is the year's Books as Regal's row and a quiet Year in review › link, nothing else**: the row stands in the sheet's own surface (`ShelfRowCard bare`: the Profile's card size, but no surface, border or shadow; a drag that starts on it is never the sheet's swipe down), and a Book tapped breaks out **above the sheet** (the row's `--regal-row-z-index` follows `--shelf-row-z`, 55 here, over the sheet's 50 and under a dialog's 60). **Everyone else** gets the year's finished reads newest first (the Profile's rows: cover, title, author, stars · day · days it took, a read-again marked; each opens its book page with the cover flight and closes the sheet) and the same link. The owner sees the list only while her row is not there (the library file holds none of that year's Books, or could not be read). The link goes to `/profile/<year>` (`home.yearInReview`). The reading record is asked for each time the sheet opens (offline with none: `profile.offline`; a failure: `profile.loadError` and Try again). Nobody but the owner renders the row, so nobody else fetches the library file or Regal's chunk. System Back with a Book out puts the Book back and leaves the sheet; the next Back closes the sheet (`useBackDismiss` lets a Back off the row's own entry on top of the sheet's pass the router by). **A Book opened from the list and Back from it (the system's or the book page's own) is Home with the sheet open again**, as it was left: at its resting place with its scrim, the list scrolled where it was, nothing rising or fading in, the cover flying back into its row in the sheet (`composables/useSheetRestore.ts`, `restore` on `UiSheet`, `keptSheet` in `stores/stats.ts`; docs/MOTION.md, A sheet comes back). Opened any other way (the tally tapped again, another tab and back), it rises and starts shut.
States: loading (first visit: nothing yet) · empty Library, a new member (the lamp over the empty shelf: `home.emptyTitle`, `home.empty`, the search prompt `home.search`, and under it the import offer, see *Import offer on Home*; no tally, no sections) · otherwise the three blocks above (a Library with only finished Books shows `home.readingEmpty` and the tally) · load error (`home.loadError`, Retry)
Actions → result:
- Tap the search prompt → the search overlay opens over Home (the URL stays `/`).
- Tap a card's cover or title, or a Want to read cover → the book page `/book/<id>`, started on touch-down (`UiPressLink`).
- Finish (Update → Finish in the Update progress sheet, or `home.finish` on a card at the last page) → the Finish sheet over Home; once the sheet has left, the card fades and its room closes (the cards below slide up) and the tally goes up by one, the new mark lighting up in the lamp colour before it settles (the count is asked for again, one call). A Start or DNF on the book page shows the same way when Home is back on screen.
- Update (`home.update`) → the Update progress sheet; after a save, Undo (`home.undo`) for 5 s (see *Reading progress*).
- Tap the tally (with at least one Book read) → the *Read in sheet*.
- See all (a 44 pt tall target) → `/library` (Want to read is its first segment).
Data: the three Status lists as in the Library (`library.load()`), and **one count call** for the tally: `readInYear(year)` (`app/data/library.ts`) counts `reading_sessions` with `outcome = 'finished'` and `ended_on` from `YYYY-01-01` to `YYYY-12-31`, as a head request with an exact count. No migration: the table's RLS (`reading_sessions_own`) already limits it to the member's own entries.
Edge cases: the page is kept alive: coming back (from a book page, another tab) shows what was there and refreshes the lists and the count in the background, **unless they are fresh** (not asked again within 60 s of the last answer, as the Library's). A start, finish or add moves the card (`library.entryChanged`), on screen once no sheet is (`useSettled`); a finish on this device asks for the count again, the latest answer wins; only a mark added to a count already shown lights up. Signing out (or another member signing in) forgets all of it. Without a name the greeting is the plain one; D's "Welcome, <name>" for a new member is the plain greeting here too.
Copy keys: `home.greeting.morning|afternoon|evening|morningNamed|afternoonNamed|eveningNamed`, `home.today`, `home.emptyTitle`, `home.empty`, `home.reading`, `home.readingEmpty`, `home.upNext`, `home.seeAll`, `home.readIn`, `home.readInLabel`, `home.yearInReview`, `shelf.year.rowLabel`, `profile.offline`, `profile.loadError`, `profile.retry`, `home.importOffer.*`, `home.loadError`, `home.retry`, `book.since`, `book.finish`, `common.dayMonth`, `common.dayMonthYear`, `common.etAl`
IDs: `home.date`, `home.title`, `home.emptyTitle`, `home.empty`, `home.search`, `home.importOffer`, `home.importOfferTitle`, `home.importOfferText`, `home.importOfferAction`, `home.importOfferDismiss`, `home.reading`, `home.readingCount`, `home.readingCard`, `home.entry`, `home.entryTitle`, `home.entrySince`, `home.update`, `home.undo`, `home.finish`, `home.readingEmpty`, `home.tally`, `home.tallyLabel`, `home.tallyCount`, `home.ticks`, `home.upNext`, `home.upNextRow`, `home.upNextEntry`, `home.seeAll`, `home.nextInSeries`, `home.nextMore`, `homeSeries` (and the rest of *Next in your series*), `homeTally`, `homeTally.sheetTitle`, `homeTally.cancel`, `homeTally.shelfRow`, `homeTally.list`, `homeTally.read`, `homeTally.yearInReview`, `homeTally.loadError`, `homeTally.retry`, `home.loadError`, `home.retry`
Flow: no flow (the empty Home's first read is the core loop in `web/e2e/core-loop.spec.ts`; the import offer is in *Import offer on Home*; the owner's shelf and the sheet's return from a Book are not critical paths); the year rule in `web/tests/read-in-year.test.ts`, Want to read newest first in `web/tests/library.test.ts`
Native: a scrolling page; the Want to read row is a horizontal list; the tally marks are drawn, not text.

### Profile  (web: `app/pages/profile/index.vue`, `components/profile/*`, `composables/useFigures.ts`, `stores/stats.ts`, `data/stats.ts`)
Purpose: the member's reading in figures and covers, by year or all years, and her account (issue #78; design round #78, direction D: B's place and hero over A's figures). Replaces the avatar menu.
Layout: a pushed screen in the tab layout (the round back button over the page, the tab bar stays and steps away while scrolling down), lit by a cover (`UiAmbient`): the favourite of the year in view, this year's under All. Top to bottom: the **hero** — the initials in a ring the width of a `coverMd` cover, the name (`profile.title`; the address while she has none), `profile.since` ("Reading here since January 2023", the earliest start or end of a closed read), and the Library in one mono line (`profile.library`: Books finished at least once · being read · Want to read). The **year pills** (`profile.years`): All first and lit by default, then each year with a finished read, newest first; no scroll bar. The **four figures** between hairlines (`profile.figures`): Books (finished reads, re-reads included; under it "N read again · N DNF"), Pages (the member's own page count first, else the edition's; five figures and up compact, "30.1K"; under it "N without a count" (a button where there is one to open them), or "N a book"), Average (stars to one decimal; under it "N not rated yet", or "N rated"), Days a book (the median from start to finish, both days counted). **By year** (All) or **By month** (a year) as columns (always the same height, so switching years moves only the bars, nothing under the chart), the count over each, the label under it, this year / this month in the lamp colour; in a year, **"<year> in review ›"** (`profile.inReview`) beside the title. **Reading days** (All and this year, once any day of progress is kept): five Monday-first weeks up to today as dots, larger the more pages that day read, today in the lamp colour, "since <first day kept>", and two figures: days read of the last 30 ("14/30") and pages on a day read; a day that was read is a button (its dot unchanged in size, the button the cell and half the gaps around it), a day not read a quiet dot. **Ratings**: a row per whole star, 5 down to 1 (4.75 counts as four), a bar in the star colour, the count; "N rated · N not yet" over them. **Records**: Longest, Shortest (pages), Quickest, Took its time (days), each a Library row with the label over the title and the figure at the right. **Authors you return to** (read more than once, re-reads count): a fan of up to three covers, the name, a tally mark per read, the count large and the average. **Years in review**: a card per year (the year large, its count, its favourite's cover and title, lit by it). **Friends** (social v1, before the Reading page row): the eyebrow `friends.section` over four rows: **Your circle** (`profile.circle`, stack icon, → the feed), **People** (`profile.people`, its value `friends.peopleRequests` only while a request waits, → People), **Your follow link** (`profile.followLink`, → the link sheet) and **Privacy** (`profile.privacy`, its value `friends.private` or `friends.public`, → the Privacy sheet). **Account**: the address over grouped rows — Photo (see Profile photo), Name, Dark mode, Import books, Sign out. The hero's ring shows her photo where she has one and opens the same. Wide screens: the same column, centred.
States: loading (the whole page at its final height: the hero's two lines, the pills after All, every figure, bar, day, rating count, record, author and year card a placeholder in a slow wave; `aria-busy` on the figures' region; sections that may be absent guessed present — four years, four authors, Reading days, Ratings, Records — and closed smoothly if the record has none; a member whose Library on the device has nothing finished gets the hero and account only, as before) · content (the device's last record at once, refreshed by the load: only a first visit on the device shows the loading state) · empty (nothing finished yet: `profile.empty.title|text` instead of the figures) · load error (`profile.loadError` with **Try again**) · offline before anything loaded (`profile.offline`; the account rows work; with a record kept on the device, that record) · a sheet open (a month's books, a star row's, the reads without a count or a day's).
Actions → result:
- Open (the avatar in any tab's header) → `/profile`, the avatar growing into the Profile's ring as the page cross-fades and rises (Back: the other way; docs/MOTION.md, Push to the Profile); the record loads (each time it opens): **five calls, nothing written** — the closed reads with their Books and entries' own page counts (`reading_sessions` with `library_entries!inner(page_count_override, books!inner(…))`, outcome not null; the Books without their description, `LIST_BOOK_COLUMNS`), the counts of Want to read and Currently reading (head counts), the days of progress of the last 42 days (`reading_progress_days` with each day's read, its Book and its entry's own page count), and the first day kept. Every figure is worked out from those rows in `data/stats.ts` (`figuresOf`, `yearsOf`, `readsInMonth`, `readsWithStars`, `readsWithoutPages`, `readingDaysFromRows`, `readsOnDay`, `readingSinceOf`), so a native client computes the same ones.
- The year pills are one row however many years there are: it scrolls sideways (finger, trackpad, Shift + wheel) without a scroll bar, edge to edge, its scroll never carried on into the page's; the picked year scrolls into view.
- Tap a year pill → every figure, column, rating, record and author below shows that year (a year is the year a read ended, re-reads included, like *Read in <year>*; a finished read without an end date counts in All only). The pick is kept while she opens a book or a year and comes back, and goes back to All when that year has no finished read any more.
- Tap a month's column (a year) → a sheet (`profileReads`, title "May 2025") with that month's finished reads in the order they ended: cover, title, author, "Second read" on a re-read, stars (or "Not rated"), the day it ended, "in 12 days". An empty month is a dot and does nothing.
- Tap a year's column (All), "<year> in review", or a year's card → that year in review (`/profile/<year>`).
- Tap a star row → a sheet ("5 stars · 2025", or "· All years") with the reads rated so, best first, then newest; the day carries its year under All. A row with 0 does nothing.
- Tap the Pages card's **"N without a count"** line (a button only when there is one) → the same sheet (`profileReads`, title `profile.sheet.pagesMissing`, "Books without a page count") with the reads of the year in view whose Book has no page count, in the order they ended, `readsWithoutPages` — the very reads the figure counts, so the line and the sheet can never disagree; under All each row's day carries its year. A row opens its book page, where the page count can be set (#60), as any row in a sheet does. With every finished Book of the year counted, the line stays the text it was and nothing opens.
- Tap a day in the **Reading days** that was read → a sheet (`profileReads`, title `profile.sheet.day`: the day in the member's words, "Thursday 12 March") with the books read that day: the very reads whose days of progress made the dot (`readsOnDay`; a read still going included, which the closed reads would miss), so a day that was read always has at least one, as the other sheets' rows (a re-read marked, stars or "Not rated", the day it ended if it has). A row opens its book page, and Back is the sheet again. The day's button is named by its day and its pages (`profile.days.dayRead`: "12 March, 40 pages"; `dayReadNoPages`, "12 March, read", for a day read by percent alone); a day not read is not a button and nothing opens. The grid's own name stays (`profile.days.label`, a group).
- Tap a read in a sheet, or a record → its book page (the sheet closes with the page change); Back (the system's or the book page's) is the Profile, the year still picked and **the sheet open again** (`composables/useProfileSheet.ts` over `composables/useSheetRestore.ts`, `keptSheet` in `stores/stats.ts`), as if it had stayed open: at its resting place with its scrim, its list scrolled where it was, nothing rising or fading in, the cover flying back into its row in the sheet. Opened any other way, the Profile starts without a sheet and a sheet opened by hand rises.
- Friends rows: Your circle → `/friends` (see *The feed*); People → `/friends/people` (see *People*); Your follow link → the link sheet (see *Follow link*); Privacy → the Privacy sheet (see *Privacy*).
- Photo, or the hero's ring → see Profile photo. Name → the name sheet (see Member name). Dark mode → see Theme. Import books → `/import`. Sign out → see Tab shell. Delete account (the last row, danger colour) → see Delete account.
- **Glass** (the owner's account only, like Errors; the row under Dark mode, stack icon): a small segmented choice **Strong · Medium · Off** at the row's right (`UiSegmented compact`: 32 high, footnote text, each option's touch target 44), setting how glassy the floating chrome is on this device: the tab bar, the avatar menu, the round buttons and the search veil. **Strong** is the design (heavy glass: blur 30 / 24 / 7, the tint 58–64 % opaque, the page showing through); **Medium** halves every blur radius and takes the glass tint 55 % (the veil 45 %) of the way to the room's solid colour; **Off** is no transparency at all: those surfaces are solid in the room's own colour (the sheet colour for the glass, the surface colour for the veil), with no backdrop blur, still hairline-edged and readable, in light, dark and sepia. Stored on the device (`libellus-glass`: `strong|medium|off`; the first version's `full|light` read as `strong|medium`; kept through sign-out, like the theme) and put on the page as `data-glass` on `<html>` at boot (`utils/glass.ts`, `stores/glass.ts`). Also what the blur costs on a phone while things move behind it. Native: the system's own material at Strong, a thinner one at Medium, an opaque fill at Off (iOS: Reduce Transparency does the same).
- Back (the round button) → where it was opened from (by history), else Home.
Edge cases: everything is read through RLS: only her own reads, entries and days; the reads and days come page by page (the API answers 1,000 rows a request), so an imported history of any size counts whole. The 12 month columns are narrower than 44 (the full height is the target): an accepted trade-off for a year at a glance. A read without both dates has no days; a Book without any page count is left out of the pages and counted "without a count", which the Pages line opens (a re-read without a count counts and opens like any other; an abandoned read never does). Reading days show only once some day of progress is kept (`reading_progress_days` is new with #68/#74: older reads have none) and never count a run: which days and how much, no streaks, no goals. A day's button is as large as the grid has room for (24 × 20: its cell and half the gaps, never overlapping the next) because the dots must not shrink or the grid move; the record kept on a device from before the days named their reads shows plain dots until the next load. No sentences: the owner turned down text summaries. Offline nothing is asked for; the last record stays on screen.
Copy keys: `profile.title|back|since|library|yearsLabel|all|byMonth|byYear|inReview|columnLabel|loadError|offline|retry`, `profile.figures.*` (`pagesMissing`, and `pagesMissingOpen` for the line's accessible name), `profile.days.*` (`dayRead`, `dayReadNoPages` for a day's button), `profile.ratings.*`, `genre.stats.*` (the Genres block, see *Genres*), `profile.records.*`, `profile.authors.title`, `profile.years.*`, `profile.account.title|theme|glass|signOut`, `profile.account.glassLevel.*`, `profile.empty.*`, `profile.sheet.*` (`pagesMissing` for the reads without a count, `day` for a day's title), `history.ordinal.*`, `history.nth`, `rating.none`, `shell.avatarLabel`, `account.name|nameNone`, `import.menuItem`, `friends.section|people|peopleRequests|followLink|private|public`, `circle.title`, `privacy.title`
IDs: `profile`, `profile.back`, `profile.hero`, `profile.initials`, `profile.title`, `profile.since`, `profile.library`, `profile.years`, `profile.year.all|<year>`, `profile.figures`, `profile.books`, `profile.booksLine`, `profile.pages`, `profile.pagesMissing`, `profile.average`, `profile.daysABook`, `profile.inReview`, `profile.columns`, `profile.columns.<month|year>`, `profile.days`, `profile.day.<YYYY-MM-DD>`, `profile.daysRead`, `profile.pagesADay`, `profile.ratings`, `profile.stars.<1–5>`, `profile.records`, `profile.record.longest|shortest|quickest|slowest`, `profile.readTitle`, `profile.readAgain`, `profile.authors`, `profile.author`, `profile.authorName`, `profile.authorCount`, `profile.yearCards`, `profile.yearCard.<year>`, `profile.account`, `profile.email`, `profile.name`, `profile.nameValue`, `profile.theme`, `profile.glass`, `profile.glass.strong|medium|off`, `profile.import`, `profile.signOut`, `profile.empty`, `profile.loadError`, `profile.retry`, `profileReads`, `profileReads.cancel`, `profileReads.sheetTitle`, `profileReads.list`, `profileReads.read`, `profile.friends`, `profile.circle`, `profile.people`, `profile.peopleValue`, `profile.followLink`, `profile.privacy`, `profile.privacyValue`

### Your circle on Home  (web: `components/home/Circle.vue`, `components/home/CircleFeature.vue`, `components/home/CircleFriend.vue`, `components/friends/RequestRow.vue`, `utils/circleView.ts`; last on `app/pages/index.vue`, also under an empty Library's state)
Purpose: a glance at what the people she follows are doing, and the place to answer a follow request (social v1, the owner's combined design; replaces the three feed rows of the first design).
Layout: top to bottom: the eyebrow **Your circle ›** (`circle.title`), a link to the feed; the newest follow request as one raised row (avatar with a lamp dot, name, `circle.askedLine` "asked to follow you", **Accept** and a ✕); one card for the newest finish or review of the last 7 days that is not part of a batch (cover, avatar, name, "finished" or "reviewed", the title in the serif, stars, the review in serif italic folded at three lines with **More**), lit by the cover (`UiAmbient`; the cloth's colour for a Placeholder); up to three rows, one per member, newest first: avatar, name, a day eyebrow, one sentence of her two newest events joined by " · " (two events of one kind read as one phrase: "added *Dune* and *Piranesi* to Want to read"), titles in the serif italic, a fan of at most three covers; **Show more** (`circle.more`) to the feed. The card's Book leaves the list; the member's other activity still makes her row.
States: hidden altogether with no request, no card and no row (Home does not nudge anyone to be social) · request alone · card and/or rows · no card when nothing was finished this week · offline: the request's buttons become one disabled **Offline**, the rest is the device's copy.
Actions → result:
- Accept (`circle.accept`) → `social.answer` (accepted): she gets a follower; the row leaves, the lamp dot with it. Decline (✕, `circle.declineLabel`) → declined; nobody is told.
- The card's cover or title → `/book/<id>`, the cover flying there; a Manual book opens nothing.
- The card's avatar or name, or a row → `/friends/<member>`.
- More (`circle.more` on the card, `feed.more`) → the review unfolds.
- The title (`circle.title`) and Show more → `/friends`.
Edge cases: a card is the newest finish or review of the last seven days (`CARD_DAYS`) and is never part of a batch; at most three member rows (`CIRCLE_MEMBERS`), two phrases per row (`CIRCLE_PHRASES`), three covers per fan (`CIRCLE_COVERS`). Shown under the empty Library's state too, for a new member with a request or activity.
Copy keys: `circle.title|more|askedLine|accept|declineLabel|want|join|and`, `feed.finished|reviewed|started|startedAgain|abandoned|want|batchFinished|batchStarted|batchAbandoned|batchWant|batchReviewed|more|today|yesterday`, `member.someone`
IDs: `home.circle`, `home.circleTitle`, `home.circleRequest`, `home.circleAccept`, `home.circleDecline`, `home.circleFeature` (+ `.member`, `.cover`, `.title`, `.stars`, `.review`, `.more`), `home.circleFriend`, `home.circleMore`
Flow: `web/e2e/friends.spec.ts`; `web/tests/circle-view.test.ts`

### The feed  (web: `app/pages/friends/index.vue`, `components/friends/FeedRow.vue`, `components/friends/FeedBatch.vue`, `data/feed.ts`, `stores/feed.ts`; database: `feed()`)
Purpose: what the people she follows started, finished and added, newest first (social v1; "Your circle" in full).
Layout: a pushed screen in the tabs layout: round Back at the left, **People** at the right (`friends.people`), the large title "Your circle" (`feed.title`); day eyebrows (`feed.today`, `feed.yesterday`, a weekday within six days, else the date); the rows and batches (see *A batch*). A row: the cover, the member's avatar and name with the verb, the title in the serif, authors, the quarter-star rating, and her review folded at four lines with **More**. Verbs: started, started again, finished, didn't finish, added to Want to read, reviewed. A review written after a finish is its own row ("reviewed"). A rating changed later shows as the current stars, never as an entry of its own.
States: loading (the device's copy at once and refreshed; with no copy, row placeholders) · content, 30 entries at a time (`FEED_PAGE`), older pages as she scrolls near the end · pull to refresh · empty, following nobody: `feed.emptyTitle` "Reading is better with a friend or two.", `feed.empty`, and **Share your follow link** (`feed.emptyShare`, to the link sheet) · empty, following people with nothing yet: `feed.quietTitle` / `feed.quiet` "Quiet for now." · offline: the last copy the device kept, under `feed.offline` "Offline · as of {time}" (a copy older than seven days is not shown) · load error: `feed.loadError` with **Try again** (`feed.retry`).
Actions → result:
- A cover or title → `/book/<id>` with the cover flight; a Manual book opens nothing.
- The name or the avatar → `/friends/<member>`.
- A batch → its sheet (see *A batch*).
- People → `/friends/people`.
- Back → Home (the page is pushed from Home's *Show more*).
Edge cases: never an entry: imports (Goodreads, Hardcover, the owner's Fable import); a finish or DNF logged with an end more than 14 days back; anything in its first 10 minutes (a correction inside them replaces it); a hidden Book (*Hide from followers*); a switched-off section. Entries of a member the viewer does not follow never show. Offline nothing is asked for.
Copy keys: `feed.*` (as listed under *Your circle on Home*, plus `feed.title`, `feed.peopleLabel`, `feed.emptyTitle|empty|emptyShare|quietTitle|quiet|offline|loadError|retry`), `friends.people`, `member.back`
IDs: `friends`, `friends.title`, `friends.back`, `friends.people`, `friends.day`, `friends.entry`, `friends.entryMember`, `friends.entryBook`, `friends.entryStars`, `friends.entryDay`, `friends.entryReview`, `friends.entryMore`, `friends.empty`, `friends.emptyShare`, `friends.quiet`, `friends.offline`, `friends.loadError`, `friends.retry`
Flow: `web/e2e/friends.spec.ts`; `web/tests/feed.test.ts`, `web/tests/feed-view.test.ts`

### A batch  (web: `components/friends/FeedBatch.vue`, `components/friends/BatchSheet.vue`, batching in `data/feed.ts`)
Purpose: three or more of the same kind by the same member on the same day, as one row of the feed.
Layout: one row: a fan of covers, the member's avatar and name, "finished 3 books" (`feed.batchFinished`; likewise `batchStarted`, `batchAbandoned`, `batchWant` "added 3 to Want to read", `batchReviewed`), the day eyebrow. The sheet (`friendsBatch`) is titled "<name> · <count>" (`feed.batchTitle`), lists the day and one row per Book (cover, title, authors).
Actions → result:
- The row → the sheet (`friends.batchOpen`).
- A Book in the sheet → `/book/<id>` with the cover flight; Back from the Book brings the sheet back as it was. A Manual book opens nothing.
Edge cases: exactly three is a batch (`BATCH_FROM` = 3); two are two rows. Different kinds of one member on one day are separate rows. A batch shows only the Books the feed may show (hidden Books and switched-off kinds are left out before counting).
Copy keys: `feed.batchFinished|batchStarted|batchAbandoned|batchWant|batchReviewed|batchTitle`

### People  (web: `app/pages/friends/people.vue`, `components/friends/PersonRow.vue`, `components/friends/RequestRow.vue`, `components/friends/MemberSheet.vue`; `stores/social.ts`, `data/social.ts`; database: `follow()`, `answer_request()`, `unfollow()`, `remove_follower()`, `block()`)
Purpose: who she follows, who follows her, and who asked to; the place to act on each.
Layout: a pushed page in the tabs layout: round Back, the title `people.title`, and a segmented control (`UiSegmented`) of **Following** (`people.following`), **Followers** (`people.followers`) and **Requests** (`people.requests`). Requests shows only while a request waits (or while a row accepted here is still on the page), and the page opens on it then. Rows are avatar and name. Following and Followers rows open `/friends/<member>` and have a ⋯ (`people.moreLabel`) that opens the member sheet. A follower she does not follow gets **Follow back** (`people.followBack`); the answer shows at once as nothing (she follows them now) or **Requested** (`member.requested`). A request row shows `people.askedOn` (the app's relative time, "asked yesterday") with **Accept** and **Decline**; request rows do not open the profile. After Accept the row reads `people.followsYouNow` with **Follow back** until she leaves; after Decline it leaves at once.
Member sheet (`memberSheet`, a `UiSheet` titled with her name), opened from a People row's ⋯ and from the member profile's ⋯: **Unfollow** (`people.unfollow`, minus icon, when she follows the member) and **Remove as follower** (`people.remove`, close icon, when the member follows her) act at once and are silent. **Block** (`people.block`, slash icon, danger colour) asks first in `blockConfirm` (`people.blockTitle` "Block {name}?", `people.blockText`, `people.blockConfirm`); `people.sheetNote` sits under the rows: "{name} isn't told…".
States: loading · empty lines `people.emptyFollowing` "You don't follow anyone yet." and `people.emptyFollowers` "Nobody follows you yet." · load error (`people.loadError`, **Try again** `people.retry`) · write error (`people.error`, under the content) · offline: every write button is disabled and labelled **Offline** (a request row shows one Offline button).
Actions → result: Accept / Decline → *Follow requests*. Follow back → a request, or an instant follow when the other account is public (the database decides). Unfollow, Remove as follower, Block (after the confirm) → the write, the row leaves once the sheet has gone (`useSettled`).
Edge cases: limits, refused by the database: 150 follows, 20 open requests, 30 follow calls an hour (`follow_limit`, `rate_limited`). Block removes both directions, refuses her requests and link visits, and hides each from the other everywhere; the blocked member is not told. Unblock is in the Privacy sheet's *Blocked*.
Copy keys: `people.title|following|followers|requests|askedOn|accept|decline|followBack|followsYouNow|moreLabel|unfollow|remove|block|blockTitle|blockText|blockConfirm|sheetNote|emptyFollowing|emptyFollowers|loadError|retry|error`, `member.requested`, `common.offline`
IDs: `people`, `people.title`, `people.back`, `people.segment.following`, `people.segment.followers`, `people.segment.requests`, `people.row`, `people.rowMember`, `people.rowMore`, `people.accept`, `people.decline`, `people.followBack`, `people.requested`, `people.empty`, `people.loadError`, `people.retry`, `people.error`; `memberSheet` (`.unfollow`, `.remove`, `.block`, `.error`, `.cancel`); `blockConfirm` (`.confirm`, `.cancel`)
Flow: `web/e2e/friends.spec.ts`; `web/tests/people.test.ts`

### Follow requests  (web: `components/friends/RequestRow.vue`, `components/home/Circle.vue`, the Requests segment of `app/pages/friends/people.vue`, the People row's value in `components/profile/Friends.vue`, the lamp dot in `components/shell/Header.vue`; `stores/social.ts`; database: `answer_request()`, `follow()`)
Purpose: a member asks to follow a private account, and the account's owner answers. No screen of its own: the request shows in four places.
Layout, where a waiting request shows: (1) **Home**, the top row of *Your circle* (compact: avatar, name, `circle.askedLine`, **Accept** `circle.accept`, ✕ `circle.declineLabel`); (2) **People**, the Requests segment (full rows, see *People*); (3) **Profile → Friends → People**, its value `friends.peopleRequests` ("1 request", "{count} requests"), shown only while one waits; (4) the **header**: a lamp dot on the avatar while one waits, `shell.avatarDot`, the avatar named `shell.avatarRequest` ("Your profile, a follow request is waiting"). No number anywhere.
States: none waiting → no row, no segment, no value, no dot · waiting · offline (one disabled **Offline** button in each request row; the dot is not read offline).
Actions → result:
- **Accept** → `answer_request` accepted: the asker becomes a follower and her row offers **Follow back** (a request or an instant follow, by the other's privacy). The request leaves every place at once.
- **Decline** → declined; nobody is told. To the asker it stays "Requested".
- The header's avatar → the Profile (its People row carries the value).
- Follow back (after Accept, or next to any follower not followed) → see *People*.
Edge cases: the dot and the request count are read when the tab shell starts and when the app comes back to the foreground, online only (`mine.requests`). A request from a blocked member does not exist (block refuses requests). The asker's side of a declined request is not shown to her as declined (`declined_at` is never sent).
Copy keys: `circle.askedLine|accept|declineLabel`, `people.askedOn|accept|decline|followBack|followsYouNow`, `friends.peopleRequests`, `shell.avatarRequest`, `common.offline`
IDs: `home.circleRequest`, `home.circleAccept`, `home.circleDecline`, `people.segment.requests`, `people.accept`, `people.decline`, `people.followBack`, `profile.people`, `profile.peopleValue`, `shell.avatarDot`
Flow: `web/e2e/friends.spec.ts`; `web/tests/people.test.ts`

### A member's profile  (web: `app/pages/friends/[member]/index.vue`, `components/friends/{MemberHero,MemberFinished,MemberFinishedRow,MemberFinishedSheet,MemberWantSheet,MemberReview,MemberCovers,MemberSheet}.vue`, `utils/memberProfile.ts`, and the Profile's own blocks it reuses (`components/profile/*`))
Purpose: another member's reading as she lets followers see it (social v1, `/friends/<member>`).
Layout: a pushed screen in the tabs layout: round Back at the left and ⋯ (`member.more`) at the right. The hero: the large avatar (her photo, or initials), name, "Reading here since …" (`member.since`), the Library line in mono (`member.library`: read · reading · want, counted from what she shows), lit by her favourite cover. Then the **Follow** button (`member.follow`) or **Ask to follow** (`member.ask`) when she is not followed. Then, by her switches, top to bottom: **Currently reading** (`member.reading`, covers only, no progress); **Want to read** (`member.want`: the newest 12, then **See all** `member.wantAll` to the sheet `memberWant`); the year pills and the four figures; By year / By month; Ratings; Records; Authors you return to; **Recently finished** (`member.finished`: the newest three, each row a cover, title, author, stars, the day, and her review folded at four lines with **More**; **See all** (`member.finishedAll`, only above three) opens `memberFinished` with up to 12); **Years in review** (a card per year, to her year page). A section she has switched off is absent, not blank.
States: loading (placeholders) · content · not found (`member.missing`: `follow.missingTitle` "This link isn't here any more." — the same answer for an unknown, blocked or dead member) · private card (`member.private`): a lock, `member.privateTitle` "Private account", `member.privateText` "Ask to follow to see what {name} reads…", and **Ask to follow** → **Requested** (`member.requested`, `member.requestedHint` "Tap to withdraw.") · offline: `member.offline` "You're offline. This profile shows once you're back." · load error: `member.loadError` with **Try again** (`member.retry`).
Actions → result:
- **Follow** (public account, not followed) → `follow`; online only, otherwise the button says Offline.
- **Ask to follow** (private) → a request; **Requested** → tap withdraws it. Online only.
- ⋯ → the member sheet (Unfollow · Remove as follower · Block; see *People*).
- **See all** → `memberWant` or `memberFinished`, the same rows.
- A cover or title → `/book/<id>` with the cover flight; a Manual book's cover opens nothing.
- A column or a star row → the same sheets as on her own Profile, for her figures.
- A year card, or a year's column → `/friends/<member>/<year>` (see *A member's year in review*).
Edge cases: a private account's card is reachable only after her follow link was opened (`member_profile` answers null otherwise). Currently reading shows covers only. Records and Authors you return to still link a Manual book to `/book/<id>` (they carry no manual flag): a follower who taps one lands on that page's missing state, as for any other member's Manual book. A year with no Books shows only the year's title. Her reading days and progress are never shown.
Copy keys: `member.back|moreLabel|since|library|follow|ask|requested|requestedHint|privateTitle|privateText|reading|want|wantAll|finished|years|yearEyebrow|someone|offline|loadError|retry`, `follow.missingTitle`, `feed.more`, and the `profile.*` copy of the reused blocks

### A member's year in review  (web: `app/pages/friends/[member]/[year].vue`, the year blocks of `components/profile/*` reused, `utils/memberProfile.ts`)
Purpose: one year of another member's reading on a page of its own, as on her own Profile (`/friends/<member>/<year>`). Never her reading days or progress.
Layout: a pushed screen in the tabs layout: round Back (`memberYear.back`, labelled `member.back`); the eyebrow "<name> · Year in review" (`member.yearEyebrow`); the year; the figures; the months as rows of covers; the favourite; ratings; records; authors; then the years either side (`memberYear.before`, `memberYear.after`), and the page's end back to her profile.
States: loading · content · missing (`memberYear.missing`, the same missing copy as her profile) · load error (`memberYear.loadError`, `memberYear.retry`). A year with no Books shows only the year's title.
Actions → result: Back → her profile. A year either side → that year's page. A favourite cover or a Book row → `/book/<id>`, as on her profile; a Manual book opens nothing.
Edge cases: a section she has switched off is absent here too; the figures do not show Ratings when she has switched those off.
Copy keys: `member.yearEyebrow`, `member.back`, `member.someone`, and the `yearInReview.*` copy of the reused blocks

### Follow link  (web: `app/pages/f/[token].vue`, `components/friends/LinkSheet.vue` (her side), `components/profile/Friends.vue` (the row), `utils/pendingFollow.ts` and `middleware/auth.global.ts` (the device's copy); database: `follow_target(token)`, `renew_follow_link()`, `follow_link_views`)
Purpose: her link (`/f/<token>`, 128 random bits) opens her profile for whoever follows it, from her side and the visitor's.
Layout, her side (the sheet `followLink`, from the Profile's **Your follow link** row, titled `followLink.title`): a line saying whether her account is private (`followLink.textPrivate`: "…so they ask and you decide.") or public (`followLink.textPublic`: "…so they can follow you straight away."); the link in mono (`followLink.value`: the origin and `/f/<token>`); **Share link** (`followLink.share`: the platform's share sheet with `followLink.shareText`, else the clipboard); **Copy** (`followLink.copy`); a polite status (`followLink.copied` "Copied."); **New link** (`followLink.renew`, disabled and labelled Offline when offline) with the hint `followLink.renewHint` ("A new link stops the old one at once. People who already follow you stay."). The link exists from the first time she opens Friends and does not depend on the privacy switch.
Layout, the visitor's (`/f/<token>`): `follow.loading` "Opening…" while the token is resolved; then the screen is replaced by the member's profile (`/friends/<member>`) or, for her own link, her Profile. A link that does not resolve shows the missing state: `follow.missingTitle` "This link isn't here any more." and `follow.missing` "Ask for a new one."
Actions → result:
- Her **New link** (after `renewFollowLink`: `followLink.renewTitle` "Make a new link?", `followLink.renewText`, `followLink.renewConfirm`) → `renew_follow_link()`: the old link dies at once; the sheet's link changes.
- The visitor, signed in → `follow_target(token)`: the profile, and the link view is written (what lets a private account's card reach her). Her own link → her Profile.
- The visitor, signed out → the token is kept on the device for an hour (`libellus.pendingFollow`) and the link is opened after sign-in or sign-up; a waiting shared payload (a share into the app) goes first.
- Offline: nothing is asked; the visitor's link shows the loading copy until she is back online, and the sheet's **New link** is disabled.
Edge cases: unknown, renewed, blocked (either way) and malformed links give the same missing state, and nothing says why. The app's SPA fallback serves `/f/<token>`; no Pages Function is involved. The error log reports `/f/[token]`, never the token. Opened by a member she already follows, the profile is the full one (see *A member's profile*).
Copy keys: `follow.loading|missingTitle|missing`, `followLink.title|textPrivate|textPublic|value|share|shareText|copy|copied|renew|renewHint|renewTitle|renewText|renewConfirm`, `friends.followLink`, `common.offline`
IDs: `follow`, `follow.loading`, `follow.missing`; `profile.followLink`; sheet `followLink` (`.cancel`, `.action`), `followLink.value`, `followLink.share`, `followLink.copy`, `followLink.renew`, `followLink.outcome` (the status line; its copy is `followLink.copied`), `followLink.error`; `renewFollowLink` (`.confirm`, `.cancel`)
Flow: `web/e2e/friends.spec.ts`; `web/tests/pending-follow.test.ts`

### Privacy  (web: `components/friends/PrivacySheet.vue`, the row in `components/profile/Friends.vue`, `stores/social.ts`, `data/social.ts`; database: `set_private()`, `set_social_sections()`, `social_settings`)
Purpose: who sees her reading. A private account by default: each follower is let in by her. Turning it off lets any member with her link follow at once.
Layout: the sheet `privacy` (`UiSheet`, title `privacy.title` "Privacy", **Done** `privacy.done`). Top to bottom: the switch **Private account** (`privacy.private`) with its hint `privacy.privateHint` ("You approve each follower. Turn it off and any member with your link can follow you and see what's switched on below."); the eyebrow `privacy.sections` "What followers see" over seven switches: `privacy.section.reading` (Currently reading), `.want` (Want to read), `.finished` (Finished), `.ratings` (Ratings), `.reviews` (Reviews), `.abandoned` (Did not finish), `.year` (Year in review and figures); the note `privacy.never` ("Your reasons for not finishing, your progress and your notes are never shown. Hide a single Book from its ⋯ menu."); the row **Blocked** (`privacy.blocked`, value the count or `privacy.blockedNone` "None") that opens the *Blocked* sheet. A refused write shows `privacy.error` under the content; the sheet stays.
States: the database's answer for each switch (each is written one by one and shows what the database says) · offline: each switch is replaced by "Offline" (`common.offline`) and nothing is written.
Actions → result:
- **Private account** on → `set_private(true)` at once.
- **Private account** off → the confirm `goPublic` (`privacy.goPublicTitle` "Make your account public?", `privacy.goPublicText`: "Any member with your link can follow you without asking. People waiting for an answer become followers now.", `privacy.goPublicConfirm` "Make public"; `.cancel`) → `set_private(false)`; requests still waiting become followers. Cancel → nothing changes.
- A section switch → `set_social_sections` with that one key, at once; it applies to the feed, her profile and her figures, including what happened before it was switched off.
- **Blocked** → the *Blocked* sheet.
Edge cases: going private again keeps the followers she has (social-v1.md, A2). The reason she gave for not finishing never leaves her Library; the Did not finish switch shows only that she put the Book down. A Book hidden with *Hide from followers* stays hidden whatever these switches say.
Copy keys: `privacy.title|done|private|privateHint|sections|section.reading|section.want|section.finished|section.ratings|section.reviews|section.abandoned|section.year|never|blocked|blockedNone|goPublicTitle|goPublicText|goPublicConfirm|error`, `friends.privacy|private|public`, `common.offline`
IDs: `profile.privacy`, `profile.privacyValue`; `privacy` (`.cancel`, `.action`), `privacy.private`, `privacy.section.reading|want|finished|ratings|reviews|abandoned|year`, `privacy.blocked`, `privacy.blockedValue`, `privacy.error`; `goPublic` (`.confirm`, `.cancel`)
Flow: no flow of its own; the rules in pgTAP (`supabase/tests/social_settings_test.sql`, `social_follows_test.sql`)

Purpose: the members she has blocked, and unblocking them.
Layout: the sheet `blocked` (`UiSheet`, title `blocked.title` "Blocked", Cancel). One row per blocked member: her name and **Unblock** (`blocked.unblock`). Empty: `blocked.empty` "You haven't blocked anyone."

### Hide from followers  (web: the switch row in `components/book/OptionsSheet.vue` (the Book's ⋯), `setHidden` in `stores/library.ts`, `data/library.ts`, `set_entry_hidden` in `data/queuedWrites.ts`; database: `set_entry_hidden(entry, hidden)` (`library_entries.hidden`), also an action of `sync_write`)
Purpose: keep one Book out of what followers see, while it stays in her Library and her own figures.
Layout: in the Book's ⋯ sheet, right under **Share**: a switch row (lock icon) **Hide from followers** (`bookOptions.hide`). While it is on, a quiet line under the group: `bookOptions.hideHint` "Hidden: not in your circle's feed, not on your profile, not in the figures they see."
Actions → result:
- On → `set_entry_hidden(entry, true)`, shown at once. The Book is gone for everyone else: from the feed, her profile, her counts and her figures.
- Off → `set_entry_hidden(entry, false)`.
- Offline: the switch works and the change waits in the outbox like the other changes to her own Books; the sync chip counts it (see *Save offline and sync later*). It is the one follow-related write that can wait.
Edge cases: applies to any Book, Catalogue or Manual book (see *Manual book sheet*, *My edition isn't listed*). A hidden Book never becomes a feed entry. Unhiding a Book brings its old activity back with its dates, as switching a section back on does (contract §1.5a).
Copy keys: `bookOptions.hide`, `bookOptions.hideHint`
IDs: `bookOptions.hide` (the switch row)

### Year in review  (web: `app/pages/profile/[year].vue`, `components/profile/*`, `stores/stats.ts`, `data/stats.ts`)
Purpose: one year of the member's reading on a page of its own (issue #78; B's full-screen year, in figures).
Layout: a pushed screen lit by the year's favourite cover: the eyebrow `profile.year.eyebrow`, the year large (twice `text-figure`), the four figures (as on the Profile), the months as rows (`yearInReview.months`): the short month, its finished reads as covers in the order they ended, the month's count at the right (an empty month is a dash), the favourite on a lit card (`profile.year.favourite`, its cover with its glow, title, author, stars), the ratings, the records, the top three authors read more than once, and the years either side (‹ 2024 · 2026 ›).
States: loading (as the Profile's: its shape at once, a cover's placeholder in every month gone by and a dash in the months still to come, the favourite's card, ratings, records and three authors as placeholders; the owner's row its pile until the library file comes, closed if the year has no Books in it) · content · empty (a year without a finished read: `profile.empty.*`) · load error · offline before anything loaded.
Actions → result:
- Open → the same record as the Profile (loaded again).
- Tap a cover, the favourite or a record → its book page.
- Tap a star row → the sheet of the year's reads rated so.
- Tap the Pages card's "N without a count" line (as on the Profile) → that sheet for the year's reads whose Book has no page count; a row opens its book page.
- Tap the year before or after → that year **in place of this one** (the history entry is replaced), so Back is still the Profile.
- Back → the Profile (by history when it came from it, else `/profile`).
Edge cases: the year comes from the address (`/profile/2025`, four digits, anything else is not a page); any year without a finished read shows the empty state. The years either side carry their direction in their labels (`profile.year.beforeLabel|afterLabel`).
Copy keys: `profile.year.*`, `profile.figures.*`, `profile.ratings.*`, `genre.stats.*` (its top three genres, see *Genres*), `profile.records.*`, `profile.authors.title`, `profile.empty.*`, `profile.sheet.*`, `profile.back`
IDs: `yearInReview`, `yearInReview.back`, `yearInReview.title`, `yearInReview.months`, `yearInReview.month.<1–12>`, `yearInReview.read`, `yearInReview.favourite`, `yearInReview.favouriteTitle`, `yearInReview.before`, `yearInReview.after`, `yearInReview.empty`, `yearInReview.loadError`, `yearInReview.retry` (and the Profile's figure, rating and record IDs)
Flow: no flow; the figures in `web/tests/stats.test.ts`
