### Edit read sheet  (web: `components/book/EditSessionSheet.vue`, `components/ui/Confirm.vue`, `stores/history.ts`)
Purpose: fix a mistake in one read, with the rules of creating it, or delete it (issue #11; issue #1, stories 37–39).
Layout: `UiSheet` (Cancel, title `editSession.title`), the Book, a group of day rows, then by how the read ended: *Currently reading*: `editSession.startedOn` only. *Finished*: `editSession.startedOn` (placeholder `editSession.notSet`, with **Remove start date**, `editSession.clearStarted`, once set), `editSession.finishedOn`, the rating control and the review box, as in the Finish sheet. *DNF*: `editSession.startedOn` (the same), `editSession.stoppedOn` and the reason box (`editSession.reason` · `editSession.optional`), as in the DNF sheet. The error if any, the primary **Save**, and under it the destructive **Delete this read**.
States: editing (the read's own values) · busy (`editSession.busy`, controls locked) · error (`library.error.<code>` above Save, the day rows error-coloured for a day problem; the button reads `editSession.retry`) · asking to delete (see below).
Actions → result:
- Save (`editSession.submit`) → the days are checked on the device (a malformed day; one after today; an end before the start; an open read without a start; a read that had an end day losing it), then **one database call**, `update_session(p_session_id, p_started_on, p_ended_on, p_rating, p_review, p_abandon_reason)`. The edit is the whole read as the sheet shows it: leaving the Rating or review empty clears it. What the read's outcome does not allow is not sent (an open read has only its start; a finished one no reason; an abandoned one no Rating or review), and the database refuses it with `session_invalid` if it is sent. The outcome never changes by an edit. The review and the reason are trimmed (blank is none). The entry is read again and replaces the old one everywhere (`library.entryChanged`: the status line and Rating on the page, the Library lists in their new order, Home's counter), the history is read again, the sheet closes.
- **Delete this read** (`editSession.delete`) → the confirmation (`UiConfirm`, `deleteSession`): title `deleteSession.title`, `deleteSession.text`, and when it is the only read also `deleteSession.only`; the destructive button `deleteSession.action` (`deleteSession.busy` while it runs) and Cancel. Confirm → **one call**, `delete_session(p_session_id)`; the entry follows what is left (the only read gone: Want to read, with Start reading offered; an open read gone: Finished by the earlier read; an earlier read gone while a later one is open: still Currently reading). The question and the sheet close; the page shows the new state without a reload. A refusal stays in the question (`deleteSession.error`) and the same button tries again.
- Cancel, the scrim, a swipe down, Escape → closes without saving (Escape closes the question first when it is open).
Rules (database, `update_session`): a finished or abandoned read keeps its outcome; its end day is required unless it already had none (a read logged after the fact may stay undated); a start day may be removed; Rating 1–20 quarters; review up to 10,000 characters; reason up to 1,000; no day after today (wherever on Earth today is latest); end not before start.
Edge cases: another device deleting the read first → `session_not_found`. Editing the dates can change which read is the latest (the open one always is); the Library follows it.
Copy keys: `editSession.*`, `deleteSession.*`, `library.error.*` (new: `session_not_found`), `rating.*`, `common.cancel`
IDs: `editSession`, `editSession.cancel`, `editSession.scrim`, `editSession.started`, `editSession.ended`, `editSession.clearStarted`, `editSession.rating` (the slider), `editSession.rating.value`, `editSession.rating.clear`, `editSession.review`, `editSession.reason`, `editSession.error`, `editSession.submit`, `editSession.delete`, `deleteSession`, `deleteSession.scrim`, `deleteSession.title`, `deleteSession.text`, `deleteSession.error`, `deleteSession.confirm`, `deleteSession.cancel`
Flow: no flow; the repository in `web/tests/history.test.ts`, the rules and the cascades in `supabase/tests/session_history_test.sql`
Native: a sheet; the confirmation is the platform's destructive alert or action sheet.

### Remove from Library  (web: `components/book/OptionsSheet.vue`, `components/ui/Confirm.vue`, `stores/history.ts`, the ⋯ in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`)
Purpose: take a Book out of the Library entirely (issue #11; issue #1, story 40).
Layout: a round ⋯ button (`book.options`) at the right of the book page's top bar, on every Book page. It opens the sheet **Options** (`bookOptions.title`) with the Book under the title row (`UiBookLine`); for a Book in the Library: Read as, Share, Hide, then **Change edition** (`bookOptions.changeEdition`, see Change edition), the ebook rows (*Add ebook* or *Replace ebook file* and *Unlink ebook*, see Ebooks on the device) and the destructive row **Remove from Library** (`bookOptions.remove`). A Book that is not in the Library has **Change edition** only: the other rows are the entry's, and the Book line is not a row.
States: row · asking (the confirmation `removeEntry`: `removeEntry.title` with the Book's title, `removeEntry.text`, the destructive button `removeEntry.action` / `removeEntry.busy`, Cancel) · error (`library.error.<code>` in the question; the same button tries again).
Actions → result:
- Remove from Library → the sheet closes and the confirmation opens. Confirm → **one call**, `remove_from_library(p_entry_id)`: the entry is deleted and the database cascades to its reading sessions and its Collection memberships (`on delete cascade`); the Collections themselves, the other Books on them and the Book in the Catalogue stay. A Manual book stays the member's, out of reach until added again. On this device the entry leaves the Library lists, the Collections and their counts, search's marks (the result is a Book to add again) and the Home counter drops its finished reads; the page goes back where it came from (the Library when it was opened from a link). The Book can be added again as a fresh entry on Want to read.
- Cancel, the scrim, Escape → the Book stays untouched.
Edge cases: removing an entry that is gone already (another device) counts as done. Another member's entry is `entry_not_found`.

Flow: no flow; the cascades in `supabase/tests/session_history_test.sql`, the repository in `web/tests/history.test.ts`

### Error codes of the history actions
`app/data/library.ts`, `LibraryErrorCode`; copy under `library.error.<code>`. `update_session`, `delete_session` and `remove_from_library` (#11) raise the codes of the other Library actions (`not_signed_in`, `entry_not_found` for a removal, `date_invalid`, `date_in_future`, `ended_before_started`, `rating_invalid`, `review_too_long`, `reason_too_long`, `session_invalid` for a value that does not belong to the read's outcome) and add `session_not_found` (no such read in the member's Library, another member's read included; gone on another device).

### Reading progress  (web: `components/book/Progress.vue`, `components/book/ProgressSheet.vue`, `components/progress/Wheel.vue`, `components/ui/Progress.vue`, the card in `components/home/ReadingCard.vue`, `composables/useProgressText.ts`, `composables/useHoldRepeat.ts`, `data/progress.ts`, `utils/wheel.ts`, `stepTick` in `utils/haptics.ts`, `updateProgress` in `data/library.ts`, `stores/reading.ts`; database: `update_progress`)
Purpose: while a Book is Currently reading, record how far the member is, as a page or a percent; only the latest value is kept (issue #39; issue #1, Out of Scope, item 1). The sheet and the card are design round #65's direction D (issue #68): every change sits behind a button (Update), a number wheel does the entering, nothing on the card edits by accident.
Layout, book page (Currently reading only, between the status line and the page's action, **Update progress**; the bar and the line under it are always there, the rest once the read has progress, see *Progress by day*): the bar (`book.progressBar`, a `progressbar` with `aria-valuenow` in whole percent and the value in words as `aria-valuetext`: "p. 212 of 480", "45 %", "Not started yet"); then the **line** (issue #81): the value in words (`book.progressText`: "p. 212 of 480", "44 %"; before anything is tracked "Not started · 224 pages", `book.progress.notStarted`, or "Not started · No page count", or "Not started · Counting in percent" for a read stored in percent); **Update progress** (`book.updateProgress`) is not in this line but the page's one action under the whole block (lit, full width; quiet under Read now when the ebook is here); then four figures (issue #68, see *Progress by day*): **Page** 212 with "of 608" under it (`book.progressValue`, `book.progressTotal`, the dotted "of N" opens the sheet on her own total) — or, without a page count, **Read** 44 % with **Add pages** (same IDs, opens the total wheel); **Done** 35 % (or **Left** 56 % in percent; "your copy" under it while she has her own total) (`book.progressPercent`); **A day** 18 pages (`book.progressPace`, "–" without a pace); **To go** 22 days (`book.progressToGo`, "–" without a pace, 0 at the end); the last three weeks as bars (`book.progressChart`); "Last time · Yesterday · 24 pages" (`book.lastTime`; at the last page "The last page. Finished it?"), a line of its own under the chart, there only when it has something to say. Under Update progress, the **Reading log** (`book.readingLog`). The Library's card does not show progress.
Layout, Home's card (under the author: the since line, or once the read has a day of progress the pace, see *Progress by day*): a thin bar (`home.progressBar`), then one line (`home.progress`): the value in figures (`home.progressValue`) and at its right a quiet **Update** (`home.update`) that opens the sheet. Right after a save the line adds what changed in the lamp colour (`home.progressGain`, "· +24", "· −6", "· +5 %") and **Update** gives way to a plain **Undo** (`home.undo`, `aria-label` "Undo +24") for 5 s (`UNDO_MS`). At the last page (or 100 %) the line reads **The end.** (`home.theEnd`) and the lit **Finish** (`home.finish`) takes Update's place; this is the only time the card carries Finish. Update, Undo and Finish are one button that changes, so the focus the sheet gives back to Update stays on it as Undo; a visually hidden status says the save (`book.progress.saved`, "Progress saved, +24. Undo is beside it for a few seconds.").
The sheet (`progress`, titled **Update progress**; **Save** in the title row, `progress.action`, "Saving…" while it runs, "Try again" after a failed save; Cancel at the left): the Book; "Last time · Yesterday · 24 pages" (`progress.lastTime`, once the read has a day of reading before today; no chart in the sheet: the owner keeps charts to the book page); the **wheel** (`progress.wheel`) between **−** and **+** (`progress.minus`, `progress.plus`, 44 pt round, held they repeat: one, then faster, then 5 and 10 at a time); under it, at the left, **of 608 ✎** (`progress.total`, in pages: the page count that counts, the way to her own total, see *Your own page count*) or, for a Book without a page count, **Count in pages** (same ID), then how far the wheel has moved from what is stored (`progress.delta`, "+24" in the lamp colour, "−6" faint, hidden at 0); at the right **Pages | Percent** (`progress.mode.page`, `progress.mode.percent`; drawn 32 pt, 44 pt targets) only when there is a page count; then how the read ends, one group of two rows (the grouped rows' fill and hairline edge, a hairline between them inset like `UiRow`'s, the two buttons one width): the **Finish** row (`progress.finishRow`): "Finished it?" over a hint (`progress.finishHint`: "Saves your progress first.", or at the last page "That's the last page.") and **Finish** (`progress.finish`, quiet, lit at the last page); under it the **DNF** row (`progress.abandonRow`): "Not finishing it?" (`book.progress.notFinishing`) over "Stops the read where you are." (`progress.abandonHint`, `book.progress.abandonHint`) and **DNF** (`progress.abandon`, slash icon, always quiet); a failure line (`progress.failure`). Finish and DNF are only here and nowhere on the book page. No quick steps (+10 / +25 / The end were in the prototype; the owner took them out). The sheet opens in the mode of the stored value (pages when there is a page count and the stored value is not a percent) with the wheel on the stored number (0 when none; a stored value of the other kind converted through the page count).
The wheel: a drum of whole numbers (0 to the page count in pages, 0–100 in percent with a small "%"), the centre row large in thin figures on a filled band between hairlines, two rows either side turning away and fading. A finger drags it 1:1 (its own pointer handling with capture and `touch-action: none`, so neither the page nor the sheet's swipe-down takes the gesture: `data-no-swipe`); up brings larger numbers. Past an end it gives way less and less and springs back. Let go with speed and it coasts on, slowing exponentially, and lands exactly on a number (the landing number is picked at release: speed × 650 ms, decay constant 325 ms; `utils/wheel.ts`); let go still and it settles on the nearest. A mouse wheel or trackpad turns it too. A tap on the centre types the number (an input over the centre, numeric keyboard, focused within the tap, empty with the current number as its placeholder so what is typed replaces it; Enter or leaving keeps it, clamped to the ends; left empty or Escape, the number stays); a tap on a row above or below steps there. Haptics: one tick (`navigator.vibrate(8)`) for each number that passes the centre while the member moves it (drag, coast, tap, − / +), never closer than 40 ms apart (`stepTick`), so a fast fling ticks rather than buzzes; where there is no Vibration API (iOS Safari, desktops) it is silent (the iOS switch trick plays only inside a tap, never during a drag). Reduce Motion: the coasts finish at once. Accessibility: a vertical `role="slider"` (a SeekBar for TalkBack, adjusted with a swipe up or down) with `aria-valuenow/min/max`, `aria-valuetext` the value in words ("p. 22 of 480", "45 %"), the label "Page" / "Percent read", a described-by hint; ↑/→ +1, ↓/← −1, Page Up/Down ±10, Home/End the ends, a digit or Enter starts typing.
Actions → result:
- Save (the title row) → **one call**, `update_progress(p_entry_id, p_page, p_percent[, p_set_page_count, p_page_count])` with the wheel's number as exactly one of page or percent (and her total when the sheet changed it): the open session's `progress_page` / `progress_percent` (the other is cleared), `progress_updated_at` = now. The sheet closes; the book page and Home's card show the new value at once (`entryChanged`) and the card offers "+N" with Undo. Nothing changed (the same value, or 0 where none was stored, and the same total) → the sheet just closes, nothing is sent.
- Undo (Home's card, within 5 s) → **one call**, `update_progress` with the value from before the save (and her total from before, when the save changed it). A read that had no progress goes back to **none** (#104): `update_progress(p_entry_id, p_clear => true)` takes the open read's page, percent and stamp back to null (it names no value: with a page or percent it is `progress_invalid`; both null without the flag stays refused; a read with none already is left as it is; her total may travel with it). The day is booked as a return to 0, so the day of the first save has no row again. Offline the same write waits (`sync_write` forwards `p_clear`) and the queued copy shows the read with no progress meanwhile (`applyWrite`). The card shows the old value and **Update** again.
- Finish (the sheet) → the same save first (a finished read keeps its last value; nothing is sent if nothing changed), then the sheet gives way to the Finish sheet for the same entry. If the save fails the sheet stays with the reason.
- DNF (the sheet) → the same save first (an abandoned read keeps where she put it down), then the sheet gives way to the DNF sheet for the same entry (`abandonFromProgress`); a failed save stays here with the reason. Focus: the DNF and Finish sheets give it back to what opened this one (Update progress).
- Finish (the card, at the last page) → the Finish sheet over Home.
- Cancel, the scrim, Escape, swipe down (outside the wheel) → nothing is saved.
- Finish or DNF (their sheets) → the session closes keeping its progress; the page and the card no longer show it. Read again / Start again starts a new session with none.
Rules (database): progress is written only to an open session (`not_reading` otherwise); exactly one of page and percent (`progress_invalid` for both or neither); a page is never negative and never past the page count that counts (her own total, else the edition's, when there is one), a percent is 0–100 (`progress_invalid`); there is no way to clear progress, only to set it; table checks hold the same (one kind, 0 ≤ page, 0–100, `progress_updated_at` set exactly when a value is). The page-count rule is `progress_page_fits(page, page_count)`; `clamp_progress_page(page, page_count)` is the page as it must be stored for a new page count and `clamp_session_progress(entry, page_count)` applies it to all the entry's sessions: what #41 (change edition) calls when an entry's page count changes (a percent never needs it; not callable by members). The wheel cannot go past the ends, so the sheet never sends a value the database would refuse.
Offline: since #93 Save, the sheet's **Finish** and **DNF**, the card's **Update**, **Undo** and **Finish** and the book page's **Update progress** stay available and wait to sync (see *Save offline and sync later*).
Edge cases: a stored page above a newer page count is shown as stored until #41 clamps it; the wheel opens on it clamped to the page count; a cached session from before progress existed has no progress fields and shows as none; a stored page on a Book without any page count opens the sheet in percent at 0.
Copy keys: `book.progress.*` (`label`, `none`, `notStarted`, `notStartedNoCount`, `notStartedPercent`, `page`, `pageOnly`, `percent`, `update`, `updateShort`, `title`, `save`, `busy`, `retry`, `modeLabel`, `mode.page`, `mode.percent`, `pageLabel`, `percentLabel`, `wheelHint`, `less`, `more`, `gainPages`, `gainPercent`, `lossPages`, `lossPercent`, `finishedIt`, `reached`, `finishHint`, `notFinishing`, `abandonHint`, `undo`, `undoLabel`, `theEnd`), `library.error.*`, `book.finish`, `book.abandon`, `common.offline`, `common.cancel`

### Error codes of progress
`app/data/library.ts`, `LibraryErrorCode`; copy under `library.error.<code>`. `update_progress` (#39) raises `not_signed_in`, `entry_not_found` (another member's entry included), `not_reading` (no open read to record progress on) and adds `progress_invalid` (not exactly one of page and percent, a page outside 0…page count, a percent outside 0–100).

### Your own page count  (web: `pageCountOf`, `ownTotal` in `data/progress.ts`, `pageCountOverride` and `updateProgress`'s `total` in `data/library.ts`, the total in `stores/reading.ts` (`progressTotal`, `editProgressTotal`, `confirmProgressTotal`, `dropProgressTotal`), the "of 608 ✎" and the total wheel in `components/book/ProgressSheet.vue`; database: `library_entries.page_count_override`, `update_progress`, `change_edition`)
Purpose: an ebook's pages follow the font size, so the edition's page count can be wrong for the member (issue #60). She may set her own total pages for a Library entry; it is the page count wherever progress is shown or checked, and the edition's page count stays the default. **Per Library entry, not per session**: she reads the entry in one format, so the total survives Finish, Read again and Start again, and every read of the entry (closed ones too) is measured against it. The page count that counts is `pageCountOf(entry)` = her total, else the edition's, else none (any page is taken and percent is what there is).
Entry point (issue #68): in the Update progress sheet, **of 608 ✎** under the wheel (`progress.total`, in pages; `aria-label` "Total pages: 608. Change"), or **Count in pages** for a Book with no page count (percent only). Tapping it turns the same wheel into the total (`progress.totalWheel`, 1–99999, on the page count that counts, or 300 for a Book with none; typed on its centre like the other): the title row's action reads **Done**, an eyebrow "Pages in your copy" (`progress.totalTitle`) sits over it with, while she has a total, **Edition's 592** (`progress.totalDrop`; **Use percent** for a Book with no page count of its own) at its right, and the hint "Count the pages of your copy, or use your reader's total." (`progress.totalHint`) under it. **Done** hands back to the progress wheel in pages against the new total: a percent becomes the page it is of the total, a page past it becomes its last page. **Edition's 592** goes back to the edition's count (a page past it becomes its last page); **Use percent** goes back to percent (the page carried over as the percent it was of her total). A total equal to the edition's page count is none (`ownTotal`). Nothing is sent until Save.
Actions → result:
- Save → **one call**, `update_progress(p_entry_id, p_page, p_percent, p_set_page_count, p_page_count)`: the total travels in the same call as the value, so the page is validated against it; it is sent only when it changed (`p_set_page_count` true; `p_page_count` null goes back to the edition's). A total equal to the edition's page count is stored as none.
- Undo on Home's card after such a save → the value and the total from before, in one call.
- The sheet opens with her total as the page count, in pages unless a percent is stored. Switching Pages | Percent converts through the total that counts.
Rules (database): `library_entries.page_count_override` integer 1–99999 (check), null = the edition's; written only through `update_progress` (members have `select` only), which still needs an open read (`not_reading`) and the entry to be hers (`entry_not_found`); a page must fit `coalesce(override, books.page_count)` (`progress_page_fits`); a total outside 1–99999 is `progress_invalid`; a refusal changes nothing (total included). When the page count that counts goes down, every read of the entry is cut back to it (`clamp_session_progress`); setting it higher again does not restore pages. `change_edition` clamps against her total when she has one (the new edition's page count only when she has none) and leaves the total on the entry. A call that does not send the total (every call from before #60) leaves it alone: the old three-argument calls still work. A call with the total and no value changes only the total (the repository can send it; the sheet always sends the wheel's value with it).
Display: Home's card (`home.progressValue`, `home.progressBar`) and the book page (`book.progressValue` "p. 500 of 560", `book.progressPercent`, `book.progressBar`) use `pageCountOf`; the percent maths (`progressFraction`, `progressPercentOf`, `progressReachedEnd`) are given the same count. A cached entry from before #60 has no `pageCountOverride` and counts with the edition's.
Offline: as the sheet's other writes (Save reads "Offline"; Done still works, it saves nothing); the repository refuses with `offline` anyway.
Copy keys: `book.progress.totalOf`, `totalEdit` (the `aria-label` of "of N"), `totalCount`, `totalTitle`, `totalHint`, `totalEdition`, `totalPercent`, `done`; `library.error.progress_invalid`
IDs: `progress.total`, `progress.totalWheel`, `progress.totalWheelInput`, `progress.totalTitle`, `progress.totalDrop`, `progress.totalHint`
Flow: no flow; the repository and the helpers in `web/tests/progress.test.ts` (*the member's own page count*), the rules in `supabase/tests/progress_page_count_test.sql`
Native: the same picker wheel switched to the total, with Done in the sheet's title row; the total in the call that saves the page.

### Progress by day  (web: `data/progressDays.ts`, `progressDays` and `updateProgress`'s `day` in `data/library.ts`, `stores/progressDays.ts`, `composables/useReadingDays.ts`, `components/progress/Spark.vue`, the card in `components/home/ReadingCard.vue`, `components/book/Progress.vue`, `components/book/ProgressLog.vue`, `components/ui/Reveal.vue` in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`, the summary in `components/book/FinishSheet.vue`, the "Last time" line in `components/book/ProgressSheet.vue`; database: `reading_progress_days`, `update_progress`)
Purpose: how far each day of a read went (issue #68, phase 2 of design round #65's direction D), for the glance on Home, the book page's figures, chart and log, "Last time", and how the read went when it is finished. History starts with this change: nothing is backfilled.
Data: one row per read and day, `reading_progress_days(session_id, day, start_page | start_percent, end_page | end_percent)`: where the read was before the day's first update (none: no progress yet, 0) and after its last. Written only by `update_progress`, **in the same call** as the progress: a value on a day without a row adds one from the value before; on a day with a row its end moves and its start stays; a day that ends where it started (an Undo, a correction back) has no row; the total alone books nothing; the read's first value, set more than a day after the read began (a read begun before this change, or one recorded late), is where it already was, not a day's reading, and books nothing (the days count on from it). The day is the **member's own calendar day**: the app sends `p_day = isoDay()` (local); the database takes a day within one of its UTC date (every time zone) and refuses another (`date_invalid`), falls back to its UTC date when none is sent (callers from before), and books a day earlier than the read's last recorded one (a phone that crossed time zones westwards) on that last day. A lower total that cuts the page back leaves the days as they were. Rows go with their read (delete the read or remove the entry: gone); Finish and DNF keep them. Members read their own (RLS through the read's entry) and write none.
How it is read (`data/progressDays.ts`, all in the read's unit: pages with a page count that counts, else percent; a value of the other kind converted through the page count, one that cannot be is nothing): a day's amount is end − start (shown never below 0); the **pace** ("A day") is what was read over the last 14 days from the first day in them that read anything to today, divided by those days, rounded, at least 1, and only with two or more reading days; **to go** is what is left divided by the pace, rounded up (0 at the end); **Last time** is the latest day before today that read something; the **reading log** is every day that read something, newest first (the page shows 14); **how the read went** (the Finish sheet, and the card at the end) is the days from its start to its end (Finish: the day it is finished on; the card: today), both counted, and the whole book (the page count, else 100 %) spread over them.
Layout:
- Home's card: once the read has a day of reading, the since line gives way to the pace (`home.pace`, "18 a day · 22 days"; "7 % a day · 8 days"; without a pace yet, the since line's words; at the last page "12 days · 51 a day") (`home.days`); the card carries no chart, the book page's is the only one. A read with no day keeps "Since 3 Oct · day 4".
- Book page (once the read has progress, see below): the four figures, the chart (`book.progressChart`, 21 days with day letters, today lit, `role="img"` with `book.progress.chartLabel`), Last time and the log, as described under *Reading progress*. Log rows (`book.logDay`): the day ("Today", "Yesterday", a weekday within the week, else "3 Oct"), the amount in the lamp colour (`book.logAmount`, "+24", "+8 %") and where the day ended (`book.logEnd`, "p. 212", "44 %").
- Update progress sheet: "Last time · Yesterday · 24 pages" over the wheel (`progress.lastTime`).
- Finish sheet: "Read in 12 days · 51 pages a day" (or "… · 9 % a day") under the Book (`finish.summary`), following the day picked.
States: never tracked · a value without a day · days · days loading · offline (see *What the book page shows before there is progress* below).
Book page, before there is progress (issue #79; the rule is `progressShownOf`, `progressStarted` in `data/progressDays.ts`): **never tracked** = the open read has no value above 0 (none stored, or 0, which is where a read stands after an Undo of its first save) **and** no `reading_progress_days` row → the empty bar and its line (issue #81: "Not started · 224 pages"), then **Update progress**, show, and nothing else: no four figures, no chart, no Last time, no reading log, so `book.progressStats` (the figures), `book.progressChart`, `book.lastTime` and `book.readingLog` are not in the page. **The first save changes only the words and the bar's fill**: the bar and the line stay exactly where they were (the same pixels), and the figures, the chart and Last time open in below the line with a fade (`UiReveal`, #79) while Update progress and what is under it glide down; with Reduce Motion a short fade and no travel. **A value but no day row** (a first value set more than a day after the read began books no day, #68; a read from before #68; a percent-only book set by hand) → the bar, its row and the four figures (pace and days to go "–") show, but no chart and no log (nothing to draw), and no Last time. **Any day row** → everything, as before (a read lowered back to 0 after earlier days still has its rows). While the days are still being asked for (a cold open, #104): no room is held for a chart, so a read with a value shows the bar and figures (as for a value without a day) and one without stays bare; when the days arrive and there are some, the chart, Last time and the log open in below with the same fade (and when there are none, nothing moves). After a save or Undo the page keeps what it showed until the answer to "its days again" is in (`refreshing` in `stores/progressDays.ts`), so the first save brings the figures, the chart and the log in together, not one after another; if that call fails, the page settles on what the value alone says.
Motion (`UiReveal`, MOTION.md): when a part appears it opens its room over `standard` and fades in (the content under it glides down, no jump), and closes over `exit` when it goes (an Undo of the first save); "Last time" fades in alongside; the room carries `data-moving` while it moves. With Reduce Motion a short fade, no travel. Each part has its own room: the bar and figures, the chart, the log (under the actions).
Actions → result: none of its own. A save or an Undo in the Update progress sheet books the day in the same call and asks for the read's days again (one call, `progressDays([session])`); the card and the book page follow. Home's cards and the book page ask for the days of the reads they show, the asks of a moment in one call (`reading_progress_days` where `session_id` in …).
Copy keys: `book.progress.perDay`, `perDayPercent`, `days`, `paceLine`, `endLine`, `amountPages`, `amountPercent`, `dayToday`, `dayYesterday`, `lastTime`, `lastTimeLine`, `atEndLine`, `figurePage`, `figureRead`, `figureDone`, `figureLeft`, `figureDay`, `figureToGo`, `figurePages`, `figureDaysUnit`, `figureOwn`, `figureNone`, `figureAddPages`, `chartLabel`, `logTitle`, `logEnd`, `readIn`, `readPerDayPages`, `gainPages`, `gainPercent`
IDs: `home.days`, `home.pace`, `book.progressText`, `book.progressStats`, `book.progressFigures`, `book.progressValue`, `book.progressTotal`, `book.progressPercent`, `book.progressPace`, `book.progressToGo`, `book.progressChart`, `book.lastTime`, `book.readingLog`, `book.logDay`, `book.logAmount`, `book.logEnd`, `progress.lastTime`, `finish.summary`
Flow: no flow for the progress by day (`web/tests/progress-days.test.ts` holds it); the never-tracked states (a read just started, a value without a day, a stored 0, a book without a page count: no flow) and the repository and the helpers, including the visibility rule, in `web/tests/progress-days.test.ts`; the rules in `supabase/tests/reading_progress_days_test.sql`
Native: the same rows through the same RPC (send the device's calendar day); the charts drawn with the platform's chart view or plain shapes; the reading log a plain list.

### Change edition  (web: `components/book/EditionSheet.vue` (the entry's change) over `components/book/EditionPicker.vue` (the sheet itself, also the import preview's Choose edition), the row in `components/book/OptionsSheet.vue`, `stores/edition.ts`, `editionChanged` and `withCover` in `stores/library.ts`, `entryChanged` in `stores/collections.ts`, `data/editions.ts`, `workEditions` in `data/openLibrary.ts`, `changeEdition` and `setFormat` in `data/library.ts`, `components/book/FormatChoice.vue` over `components/ui/IconSegments.vue`; database: `change_edition`, `set_entry_format`, `catalogue_book_for`)
Purpose: point a Library entry at another edition of its Book (another language, cover or page count) while it keeps its reads, Ratings, reviews and Collections; also how a member fixes a wrong or missing Cover (issue #41; issue #1, Out of Scope, item 5). Design D has no edition picker; the sheet follows the collection picker: rows on a grouped fill, covers at the left, D's lamp check at the right.
Entry point: the book page's ⋯ (`book.options`) → **Change edition** (`bookOptions.changeEdition`, above Remove from Library; the only row of a Book that is not in the Library, see below).
Layout (sheet `edition`, titled **Change edition**, Cancel at the left, **Change** at the right): a hint ("Pick the edition you hold. Your reads, ratings and collections stay with the book."), then **Format of your copy** (`book.edition.copyFormat`, an eyebrow) and one line under it once she has said something ("Your copy will be saved as <format>.", `edition.formatHelp`, `book.edition.formatSaid`; a polite live line, the row's description; nothing before that) over one row of four icon segments in the reader's Margins / Line spacing style — Hardcover, Paperback, Ebook, Audiobook (`edition.format`, `edition.format.<format>`, a radio group, each named by its format, arrow keys move the choice; the chosen one on the raised surface, widening to unfold its name beside the icon, the others icons only) — showing the picked edition's format (none lit when nobody knows it), then one row per edition (`edition.candidate`, a radio): the cover (sm, at the search row's sizes, on its thumbhash or colours while it loads; the first six rows' covers load at once and at high priority, the rest lazily ahead of the scroll; a failed or blank image falls back to OpenLibrary's cover of the same ISBN; the Placeholder cover without an image), the title in the serif (`edition.candidateTitle`), the author, a mono line of language · year · pages · format (`book.formatFact.<format>`: what its source said — an Apple Books edition is an ebook, OpenLibrary's `physical_format` is read — or, on her own edition, her word on it) · publisher, whichever the edition has (`edition.candidateFacts`; the publisher is last and is the one cut when the row is narrow), and on the entry's own edition "Your edition" (`edition.current`). The entry's edition is first and picked when the sheet opens. Under the list: "Looking for other editions…" while sources answer (`edition.loading`), "Other editions didn't load." with **Try again** (`edition.failed`, `edition.retry`) when every source failed, "No other editions found." (`edition.none`) when there is nothing but the current one. Last, **My edition isn't listed** (`edition.missing`, a secondary button), which closes the sheet for the next one (see My edition isn't listed).
Which editions (data/editions.ts): OpenLibrary's editions of the Book's work (`/works/<key>/editions.json`, asked 100 at a time with `offset` up to 300: a work can have hundreds, and the owner's 2022 Gollancz *I Am Legend* is the 53rd of its work's 55, so asking for the first 50 missed it), by the Book's work key, or, without one (an Apple or imported Book), by the work OpenLibrary files its ISBN-13 under; plus a search for "<title> <first author>" in the own Catalogue, Apple Books and OpenLibrary that keeps only editions of the Book (the same title, accents and punctuation aside, whoever is credited; or the title within the other's — "Im Haus der Feinde" / "Red Rising - Im Haus der Feinde" — by the same first author). One row per edition: merged by ISBN-13 (an ISBN-10 converted) and source ids; a Catalogue Book is shown as it is (it is what the entry will point at); otherwise Apple's snapshot leads and OpenLibrary fills in what it lacks (pages, language), source ids never mixed. Order: the current edition, then editions with a cover image, then those in the current edition's language (a Book without one, an Apple edition: the device's first language), then Catalogue → work editions → Apple → OpenLibrary. **Rows that look the same are one** (#47; `editionSignature`: normalised title and authors, language, year, pages, publisher, format, cover image — an Apple host `is1-ssl`/`is3-ssl` counts as one): the richest of them stays (most fields filled, the Catalogue's on a tie), and a candidate that looks like the current edition is not a row at all. At most 60 besides the current one. The member's Manual books never appear. Sources are never named; the list grows as each answers (each source asked once per opening, all aborted when the sheet closes). **Rows keep their places once shown** (`appendEditions`): the first answer is sorted, later answers only add rows at the end (sorted among themselves) and fill in details of a row already shown (a language, a page count) without moving it, so a tap never lands on another edition; rows are keyed by position, and the pick follows any edition key of its row.
Motion (docs/MOTION.md, "Change edition's list"): the list is a `UiListMotion`; an edition that arrives from a source opens its room and fades in over `standard`, the rows after it (and the line and button under the list) glide down, the current row never moves; the line under the list (`edition.loading|failed|none|offline`) is a `UiReveal` that opens and closes its room instead of popping; a sheet that opens again starts its list afresh. With Reduce Motion the list simply changes.
States: loading (the current edition and what has answered) · list · picked another (the lamp check moves, **Change** lights) · changing ("Changing…", rows disabled) · refused (the reason under the picked row, `edition.error`, the sheet stays) · nothing else · every source failed · offline.
Actions → result:
- Tap a row → it is picked (`aria-checked`); picking the current edition again disables **Change**; the Format row shows the picked edition's format and forget one she had said for another row.
- Tap a Format segment → she says the picked edition is that format: it is her word on what her copy is, never a filter (the list does not change); the picked row's facts say it at once and the help line says what will be saved. With another edition picked and a format other than its source's said, the action reads **Change & save** (`book.edition.changeAndSave`) instead of **Change**. With another edition picked her word goes with the change (`change_edition`'s `p_format`; with none said, the format the row showed is sent, so what she saw is what she gets, and the database drops it where it is the Book's own); with her own edition picked the action becomes **Save** (`book.edition.save`, "Saving…") and `set_entry_format(p_entry_id, p_format)` keeps only her word on it; a format equal to what the source says is stored as none. It is the entry's (`library_entries.format_override`), never written into the shared Book; the Book page's facts show the format that counts.
- **Change** (`edition.action`) → a Book not in the Catalogue yet gets its Cover resolved first, by the chain Add uses (Apple artwork → Apple by ISBN → OpenLibrary by cover id or ISBN → Placeholder cover; `withCover`) → **one call**, `change_edition(p_entry_id, p_book)` → the sheet closes and the page takes (replace, no new history step) the new Book's address `/book/<id>`, staying the same page where it was scrolled (`utils/bookPageKey.ts`; it follows the entry in the same render as the Library learns of it, so the old Book is never shown as not in the Library); the old cover, title, author, facts and description lie over the new ones and cross-fade into them over `standard` once the sheet has fallen away and the new cover's image is decoded (at most twice `sheet`), the page's light turning from the old cover's colours to the new one's and the content below sliding to its new place (Reduce Motion: the cross-fade alone; docs/MOTION.md, Change edition; `composables/useEditionChange.ts`); status line, history and Collections are the entry's and stay; the Library lists, the Collections showing the entry and search's results learn the new Book (`editionChanged`, `collections.entryChanged`); the old Book's page shows it as not in the Library.
- Cancel, the scrim, Escape, swipe down → nothing changes; lookups in flight are aborted.
Rules (database, `change_edition`): only the member's own entry (`entry_not_found`, another member's included); the Book is found or added exactly as `add_to_library` does it (`catalogue_book_for`, which `add_to_library` now calls too: ISBN-13 first, then Apple id or OpenLibrary edition key, else the snapshot enters the Catalogue; `book_invalid` for a Manual or key-less snapshot or an `import` snapshot matching no Book); the same edition changes nothing; an edition she already has as another entry is refused (`edition_in_library`), and the refusal leaves no new Book behind; the entry keeps its id, reads, Ratings, reviews, Collection memberships, import key, added date and derived Status; `edition_changed_at` is set; every session's progress page past the new page count is cut to it (`clamp_session_progress`, #39), a percent or a Book without a page count keeps what it has; the old Catalogue Book stays (shared); **her Read as is preset from the new edition's format** (see *Read as*, Edge cases). **A Manual book switches to a Catalogue edition too, and the Manual book it leaves is deleted** once no entry uses it (only her own entry could): kept, it would be an unreachable private copy that her search still finds next to the new edition.
**A Book not in the Library** (`edition.browse`, `viewing` in `stores/edition.ts`): the same sheet and the same list for a Book on a page with no entry, to look at the other editions. The hint reads `book.edition.browseHint` ("Look at the other editions of this book. Nothing is saved: your Library stays as it is."), the shown edition is first, marked **Showing now** (`book.edition.shown`) and picked, the action reads **Show** (`book.edition.show`), lit once another is picked, and works offline as far as the list is there (the ⋯ row is disabled offline, as it looks editions up). There is no Format row (it is her word on her copy) and no **My edition isn't listed** (it makes her an entry). **Show** writes nothing: no entry, no Catalogue Book, no cover resolved, nothing in the outbox. It sets a view of the page (`edition.viewing`, by the page's address): cover, title, facts (year, pages, format, publisher) and description are the picked edition's, with the same cross-fade as a change of edition, and **Add to Library** adds the edition shown (an Apple or OpenLibrary row is shown as the snapshot the sources gave, as a search result's page is). The view goes with the page (leaving it, or another Book's address) and is never kept. If the picked edition is in her Library already, or is added from the page, the page is that entry's, taking its address as after a change of edition. Tests: `web/tests/edition-browse.test.ts` (no write, view per page, reset).
Fable import: an entry whose edition the member changed (`edition_changed_at` set) keeps it when `scripts/import-fable.ts` runs again: its Book is neither looked up nor made again (no move back, no second Manual book); its reads still follow the record; the run reports them as `memberEdition`. The in-app Goodreads import already leaves an entry it imported before alone (`imported`).
Offline: the ⋯ row reads "Offline" and is disabled; in an open sheet the action reads "Offline" and is disabled; the repository refuses with `offline` anyway.
Edge cases: a Book with no work key and an ISBN OpenLibrary does not know (the owner's German imports) gets its candidates from the search alone; Apple's editions carry no language (#104: the iTunes Search API, search and lookup, gives ebooks no language field, checked against live answers and the recordings; only OpenLibrary's record of the same edition fills it in), so their line shows year and "ebook" (and pages and language when OpenLibrary fills them in), never a dash or an empty slot where the language would be (`editionFacts` in `data/editions.ts`: only the facts an edition has); two editions of one ISBN from different sources are one row; an edition already in the Catalogue keeps its first snapshot (the row shows that one).
Copy keys: `book.edition.*` (`open`, `title`, `hint`, `listLabel`, `current`, `loading`, `none`, `failed`, `retry`, `action`, `busy`, `save`, `saving`, `changeAndSave`, `formatLabel`, `copyFormat`, `formatSaid`, `missing`; for a Book not in the Library `browseHint`, `show`, `shown`), `book.format.*`, `book.formatFact.*`, `book.pages`, `library.error.edition_in_library` and the other `library.error.*`, `common.offline`, `common.cancel`, `common.etAl`

### My edition isn't listed  (web: `components/book/OwnEditionSheet.vue`, `components/book/FormatChoice.vue`, `stores/ownEdition.ts`, `useOwn`/`changeTo`/`setFormat` in `stores/edition.ts`, `lookupIsbn` and `isbnEdition` in `data/editions.ts`, `lookupIsbnRecord` in `data/openLibrary.ts`, `data/ownEdition.ts`, `resolveOwnCover` in `data/covers.ts`, `useOwnEdition` in `data/library.ts`, `useEdition` in `stores/import.ts`, the scanner's `pick` mode in `components/shell/BarcodeScanner.vue`; database: `use_own_edition`, `change_edition`, `move_entry_to`, `import_book_for`)
Purpose: her physical copy is not always among the editions Change edition lists (the sources know it under another work, or not at all). She finds it by its ISBN in every source, or makes it herself, and says what format it is (physical against ebook right).
Entry point: **My edition isn't listed** (`edition.missing`) under the list of Change edition (Book page) and of Choose edition (import preview). That sheet closes and this one opens for the same entry or row.
Layout (sheet `ownEdition`, Cancel at the left, the step's action at the right), three steps:
- **ISBN** (`ownEdition.isbnStep`, titled **Find your edition**): a hint (`ownEdition.isbnHint`), one field row **ISBN** (`ownEdition.isbn`, mono, the keyboard up) with a camera button at its right where the scanner works (`ownEdition.scan`, #92), then one status line (`role=status`): "Looking for this ISBN…" (`ownEdition.looking`), the wrong ISBN in the error colour (`ownEdition.isbnInvalid`), nothing found (`ownEdition.notFound`), no answer (`ownEdition.failed`), offline (`ownEdition.offline`); the primary **Look up** (`ownEdition.lookUp`; **Try again** after no answer), the step's only Look up: the sheet's header holds just its title and Cancel, and **No ISBN? Enter your edition** (`ownEdition.startOwn`, plain), which becomes the secondary **Enter your edition** once nothing was found.
- **Found** (`ownEdition.foundStep`, titled **Your edition**, action **Use**): the cover (md), the title in the serif (`ownEdition.foundTitle`), the author, a mono line of language · year · pages · format · publisher (`ownEdition.foundFacts`) and the ISBN; on the Book page the Format row (`ownEdition.foundFormat`, the source's format lit, none when it said none); the primary **Use this edition** (`ownEdition.use`) and **Not my edition** (`ownEdition.back`, back to the ISBN).
- **Her own** (`ownEdition.ownStep`, titled **Your own edition**, action **Save**): the cover she will get (her image when the link is an https URL, else the Placeholder with the Book's title and author) over a line saying title and author are the book's; **Format** with the lamp's asterisk (`ownEdition.format`, required); one row group: ISBN (the one she looked up), Year, Publisher, Pages, Language (a list of languages named in the interface's language), Cover image (an https link); the first thing wrong in the error colour (`ownEdition.invalid`, the field's label and value too), the lock and "Your edition stays out of the shared catalogue. Your followers see it with your reads; hide it from them in its ⋯ menu." (`ownEdition.private`), the primary **Save my edition** (`ownEdition.submit`) and **Back to the ISBN**.
Actions → result:
- **Look up** → a valid ISBN-10 or -13 (hyphens and spaces dropped, the check digit adding up; else `ownEdition.invalid.isbn` and nothing is asked) is asked of every source at once (`lookupIsbn`): the own Catalogue (its Book wins, never a Manual book), Apple's lookup, OpenLibrary's edition record (`/isbn/<isbn>.json`, with page count and format) together with its search by ISBN (authors, work). OpenLibrary's edition leads, being the printed edition the ISBN names; Apple's fills in a cover or description but never its "ebook" (Apple may answer with the ebook of a print ISBN); Apple's alone is an ebook; without authors the Book's are taken. Found → the Found step. Nothing → `ownEdition.notFound`. Apple and OpenLibrary both failing → `ownEdition.failed`. Typing again clears the line.
- The camera → the scanner (`scan.overlay`) over the sheet, as a layer of its own (Escape and Back close only it); a book's barcode fills the ISBN and looks it up at once.
- **Use this edition** → Book page: the entry changes to it with the format she lit (`changeTo`: Cover resolved as in Change edition, then `change_edition(p_entry_id, p_book, p_format)`; the edition she has already, found again: only `set_entry_format`), the sheet closes and the page follows as after Change edition. Import: the row takes it in the preview (`useEdition`; nothing written, the Format row is not shown: her word on a Catalogue Book would have no entry to live on yet).
- **Save my edition** → the form is checked (`validateOwnEdition`: a format; a valid ISBN or none; a year from 1 to next year; pages 1–99,999; a publisher of at most 500 characters; an https cover) → Book page: its Cover (`resolveOwnCover`: her image, with thumbhash and colours when its host allows reading it, else as it is; without one, what her ISBN has at Apple or OpenLibrary; else the Placeholder) → **one call**, `use_own_edition(p_entry_id, p_book)` → the sheet closes and the page follows as after Change edition. Import: the row takes it as a Manual book of hers, written by the import (`import_book_for` keeps its format, language and cover).
- Cancel, the scrim, Escape, swipe down → nothing changes; a lookup in flight is aborted.
Rules (database): her own edition is a **Manual book** (`source = 'manual'`, `owner_id` hers): private by RLS, never in the Catalogue or anyone else's search, found by her own search like her other Manual books, and shown to her followers with her reads like any Manual book (*Hide from followers*); its title and authors are the entry's Book's when left blank; `book_invalid` without a format or with a value the table cannot keep (pages outside 1–99,999, a language that is no 2–3 letter code, a publisher over 500 characters, a cover that is not https), `isbn_invalid` for a check digit that does not add up; a year outside 1–2100 is the table's to refuse. The move is `change_edition`'s (`move_entry_to`): her entry only (`entry_not_found`), the same row with its reads, Ratings, reviews, Collections, import key and Status; progress cut back to the page count that counts (her own total first, #60); a Manual book left behind (an earlier own edition) deleted. A format she says is `library_entries.format_override` (hers, private); one equal to the Book's is none; moving to another edition replaces it. Catalogue Books keep the format their source gave (`books.format`; Apple's are ebooks, backfilled).
Offline: Look up reads "Offline" and is disabled (the lookup needs the network); on the Book page Use and Save too, and the repository refuses with `offline`. In the import, Use and Save write nothing and work offline.
Edge cases: no photo upload for a cover yet (an https link only; a private bucket as #156's avatars would need every cover view to download it: left for later); OpenLibrary's free-text `physical_format` read in English, German, French, Italian and Spanish words (`formatFromPhysical`), CD-ROM and the like left unknown; an ISBN found that is already another of her entries is refused as in Change edition (`edition_in_library`).
Copy keys: `ownEdition.*` (`title.isbn|found|own`, `isbnHint`, `isbnPlaceholder`, `scan`, `lookUp`, `looking`, `lookingFor`, `retry`, `notFound`, `failed`, `offline`, `noIsbn`, `makeOwn`, `isbnIs`, `use`, `useLong`, `notThis`, `save`, `saveLong`, `busy`, `ownHint`, `backToIsbn`, `coverPlaceholder`, `private`, `field.*`, `invalid.*`, `error.*`), `book.edition.formatLabel`, `book.edition.missing`, `book.format.*`, `book.formatFact.*`, `manual.optional`, `library.error.isbn_invalid` and the other `library.error.*`, `common.offline`
IDs: `edition.missing`, `ownEdition`, `ownEdition.cancel`, `ownEdition.action`, `ownEdition.isbnStep`, `ownEdition.isbn`, `ownEdition.scan`, `ownEdition.looking`, `ownEdition.isbnInvalid`, `ownEdition.notFound`, `ownEdition.failed`, `ownEdition.offline`, `ownEdition.lookUp`, `ownEdition.startOwn`, `ownEdition.foundStep`, `ownEdition.found`, `ownEdition.foundTitle`, `ownEdition.foundFacts`, `ownEdition.foundFormat` (+ `.<format>`), `ownEdition.use`, `ownEdition.back`, `ownEdition.ownStep`, `ownEdition.cover`, `ownEdition.format` (+ `.<format>`), `ownEdition.year`, `ownEdition.publisher`, `ownEdition.pageCount`, `ownEdition.language`, `ownEdition.coverUrl`, `ownEdition.invalid`, `ownEdition.error`, `ownEdition.private`, `ownEdition.submit`
Flow: no flow; the lookup, the merge, ISBNs, formats, the form and the repository in `web/tests/own-edition.test.ts`; the rules in `supabase/tests/own_edition_test.sql`.
Native: a sheet with a navigation stack (ISBN → found or her own), the system's barcode scanner, a picker for the language.

### Error codes of Change edition
`app/data/library.ts`, `LibraryErrorCode`; copy under `library.error.<code>`. `change_edition` (#41) raises `not_signed_in`, `entry_not_found` (another member's entry included), `book_invalid` (as `add_to_library`) and adds `edition_in_library` (she already has that edition as another entry). `use_own_edition` raises `book_invalid` (no format, a value that cannot be stored) and `isbn_invalid`. `book_conflict` (a concurrent add rolled back) reads as `unknown`; trying again works.

### Goodreads rating on the book page  (web: `components/book/Goodreads.vue` under the facts in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`, `stores/goodreads.ts`, `data/goodreads.ts`, `BOOK_COLUMNS` in `data/library.ts`; server: `supabase/functions/goodreads-rating`; database: `goodreads_ratings`, `goodreads_rating(books)`)
Purpose (#69): Goodreads' community rating of the Book in one quiet line, so the member sees what readers made of it — any Book with an ISBN, in the Library or found in search.
Layout: in the hero, under the facts, one centred line in small mono figures, faint: the average to one decimal with a lamp-coloured ★ ("4.3★"), a dot, the number of ratings in compact notation ("137.9K ratings"), a dot, the number of written reviews ("6.1K reviews") and "on Goodreads", the word **Goodreads** underlined and linking to `https://www.goodreads.com/book/show/<id>#CommunityReviews` in a new tab (its touch area is 44 tall). Found by title (see below) Goodreads gives no review count: then "137.9K ratings on Goodreads". No Goodreads logo.
States: hidden (nothing known yet · Goodreads does not know the Book · it has no ratings · the function failed · a Book without an ISBN) · shown. The line arrives after the page: it opens its room and fades in over `standard` (MOTION.md), once the cover's flight has landed (`useAfterMotion`: never while the hero still rises); a rating already known shows at once.
Actions → result:
- The page shows → once per ISBN and app run, online only, the store asks the `goodreads-rating` edge function with the ISBN-13 (an ISBN-10 converted), the title and the authors (`createGoodreads(client).rating`, `functions.invoke`). The function answers from `goodreads_ratings` when the ISBN was checked within 30 days (a miss included), otherwise asks Goodreads by ISBN (`review_counts.json`), then, when the ISBN is unknown or only a stub without ratings, by "<main title> <first author's surname>" (`auto_complete`), matching on the normalised title (as given, without parentheses, or before a subtitle) and an author's surname; stores the answer with its time and returns it. Failures are never stored.
- Tap **Goodreads** → the Book's reviews on Goodreads, in a new tab.
Edge cases: the browser never talks to Goodreads; only the rating, the two counts and the id are kept, never review texts. The function: members only (a member's access token, or the service role), at most one Goodreads request a second per instance (a queue longer than 4 s answers `busy`), 3 s per request, one lookup per ISBN at a time. The cache is shared, keyed by ISBN-13; `books` reaches it by ISBN (`goodreads_rating(books)`, a to-one computed relationship), so every Library entry's Book carries its found rating and the device's copy of the Library keeps it: **offline, or when the function fails, the line shows the rating the Book carried**. A Book without any ISBN shows no line (nothing is looked up). Written by the service role only; members and visitors cannot write it (pgTAP).
Copy keys: `book.goodreads.ratingLabel` (the rating's accessible label), `book.goodreads.ratings`, `book.goodreads.reviews` (one | many), `book.goodreads.on`, `book.goodreads.name`
IDs: `book.goodreads`, `book.goodreadsRating`, `book.goodreadsRatings`, `book.goodreadsReviews`, `book.goodreadsLink`
Flow: no flow (`web/tests/goodreads.test.ts` holds the repository). Every flow answers the function "not found" (`web/e2e/fixtures.ts`). The function itself: `deno test` in `supabase/functions/goodreads-rating` on recorded Goodreads answers.
Native: the same function, asked the same way; the link opens the system browser.

### Book links  (web: `components/book/Links.vue` under the Goodreads line in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`, `components/profile/LinksSheet.vue` and its row in `components/profile/Account.vue`, `stores/linkTemplates.ts`, `data/linkTemplates.ts`; database: `link_templates`, `set_link_templates`)
Purpose (#116): links a member wants one tap away from every Book (a catalogue, a library, a shop), made from templates she keeps herself. Nothing is looked up or checked: they are just links, in her order.
Layout — **Book**: in the hero, under the facts and the Goodreads line, one centred line in the Goodreads line's size and quiet: the first link's label, underlined, opening the filled address in a new tab (44 tall touch area); when there are more, a dot and **More** (`book.links.more`), which opens the others in place on the same line, wrapping, each after a dot, and turns into **Less**. Hidden when there are none. **Profile**: the account row **Book links** (globe icon) with her count in mono, or `links.rowNone`, opening the sheet `links.title`: two lines on what they are and the four placeholders in mono, then one block per link, numbered (`links.first` for the one the page shows, `links.position` after), with Up, Down and Remove at the right and the fields Label and Address; **Add link** at the end (at most 20); Save at the top right.
Templates: a label (1–40 characters) and an address with placeholders, filled from the Book and URL-encoded: `{isbn}` the ISBN-13 (from the ISBN-10 if that is all), `{isbn10}` the ISBN-10 (from a 978 ISBN-13), `{title}`, `{author}` the first author. An address must start `https://` or `http://` and have no placeholder in its host; other `{words}` are refused. A link whose placeholder the Book cannot fill (no ISBN) is left out for that Book.
Two lists, shown in this order: the instance's (`NUXT_PUBLIC_LINK_TEMPLATES`, JSON built into the app, the same for every member and public like all built configuration) and hers (`link_templates`, one row per member, read only by her through RLS, written whole by `set_link_templates(list)`; deleted with her).
States: Book — none (hidden) · one · several (folded / open). Sheet — empty (`links.empty`) · editing · a field wrong (its error under it: `links.error.label_missing|label_long|url_invalid|url_long|placeholder_unknown`) · saving · save failed (`links.error.offline|link_templates_invalid|unknown`) · offline (Save reads `common.offline`, disabled).
Actions → result:
- Up / Down → the link moves one place; the first and the last can't move further (disabled). Remove → gone from the list. Add link → an empty block at the end.
- Save → empty blocks are dropped, every other one is checked; with a field wrong nothing is sent; otherwise the whole list replaces hers and the sheet closes, the row shows the new count.
- Tap a link → the filled address in a new tab. More / Less → the other links open / fold, `aria-expanded` follows.
Edge cases: her list is read once per sign-in when the Profile or a Book page opens and after each save; the device keeps the last one (`libellus.linkTemplates`, gone with sign-out), so a Book's page has her links offline. Another member never sees them (pgTAP, Vitest, the flow).
Copy keys: `links.*`, `book.links.label|more|less|moreLabel`
IDs: `book.links`, `book.link`, `book.linksMore`, `profile.links`, `profile.linksValue`, `links`, `links.action`, `links.cancel`, `links.list`, `links.item`, `links.up`, `links.down`, `links.remove`, `links.label`, `links.url`, `links.labelError`, `links.urlError`, `links.add`, `links.empty`, `links.error`
Flow: no flow; data layer `web/tests/link-templates.test.ts`; rules `supabase/tests/link_templates_test.sql`.
Native: the same table and function; the pure filling (`data/linkTemplates.ts`) ported 1:1; a link opens the system browser.

### Author page  (web: `app/pages/author/[key].vue`, `components/author/{Hero,Portrait,Credits,WorkRow,Line}.vue`, `composables/useLifeDates.ts`, `stores/authors.ts`, `stores/enrichCopy.ts`, `data/enrich/{authors,device}.ts`, `utils/enrich.ts`, `plugins/author-transition.client.ts`; the author line's links in `app/pages/book/[key].vue` (`AuthorLine`), `data-press-to` in `components/ui/PressLink.vue` used by `components/library/EntryRow.vue`; database: `author_page`, `book_authors_of`, `book_authors_for`, the `enrich` function (`refresh`))
Purpose (#167, part of #166): who wrote a Book, and everything else she wrote, with where the member stands with each.
Layout: a pushed screen: the round back button; the hero — the portrait in a ring (her initials underneath; initials alone without one), the name (`h1`), the life dates in mono (`author.lived` / `author.born` / `author.died`), the genres her works are in as quiet chips (`genre.<id>`, display only), the Wikipedia intro (cut at five lines, `author.more`), and the credits: `author.fromWikipedia` linking the article, the portrait's author (`author.photoBy`) linking the file and its licence linking the licence. Then her works, one row per work, in groups headed by an eyebrow and a count (`author.count`): each **series** in reading order (the parent series above its name: City Watch under Discworld; the most specific first), **Novels** (`author.standalone`), **Other works** (`author.other`: collections, non-fiction, works without a place). **Novels** and **Other works** list the newest first (the latest year first, works without a year last, by title between equals: `newestFirst`, a client sort); the series keep their reading order. A row: small cover, title (serif), the place and year in mono (`series.book` · 1989), and at the right her status — ✓ with her stars, Reading (the lamp), DNF, `status.want_to_read` — or **+ Want to read** (`author.want`). Long groups show eight and **Show all n**.
States: loading (the first opening on the device: placeholders of the hero and four rows in the Profile's wave, replaced whole by the page, so nothing moves) · content (at once from the device's copy, refreshed behind it) · no works (`author.noWorks`) · missing (`author.missingTitle`, `author.missing`) · offline, never opened here (`author.offlineTitle`, `author.offline`) · error (`author.errorTitle`, `author.error`, Try again)
Actions → result:
- From the Book page: each name of the author line that is one of the Book's linked authors is a link (`book_authors_of`; a single author spelled differently links too, a translator never). From a Library row: where the Book has a linked author the name is underlined, and a tap on the name itself (not the row's width) opens the page of the Book's first linked author (`book_authors_for`, the rows of a screen in one call); the rest of the row opens the Book. Search results do not: their author line sits at the row's middle, where a tap meant for the result lands, and the Book page links the author one tap later. Push: the page fades in and rises `md` over `standard`, Back the other way over `exit` (View Transitions; none with Reduce Motion).
- Tap a work → its Book's page, the cover flying (`/book/<her Book>`, else `/book/isbn-…` / `/book/ol-…` for the edition in her language).
- **+ Want to read** → the Add sheet on that edition (Want to read chosen); added, the row shows her status. Offline it is disabled and says Offline.
- A work with no edition we could add (no source has one in her language or in English) shows **Find** (`author.find`, `author.findLabel`) instead → the search opens with its title and author, as a scanned book nothing knows does; she adds the edition search finds. Offline it says Offline.
- A page older than 30 days, or whose works were never fetched (`stale`) → the `enrich` function is asked to refresh it once a session (online), then the page is read again.
Edge cases: works are one per work; a title is hers (her edition's), else in her language (the work's or its edition's), else in English, else the work's own, which the `enrich` function takes English first, then Wikidata's default label (`mul`, where items now keep a name shared by many languages: Unseen Academicals has no `en` label), then any other (`work_card`). The same order serves the series sheet and Home's row. Her own Books no source knows sit under Other works. The genres are the author's works' and her Books', canonical (#168). The device keeps the last twelve author pages (`libellus.enrich`), so they open offline; portraits are kept by the service worker (`libellus-portraits`).
Copy keys: `author.*`, `series.book`, `status.*`, `genre.<id>` (#168), `common.offline`
IDs: `author`, `author.back`, `author.hero`, `author.initials`, `author.photo`, `author.name`, `author.dates`, `author.genres`, `author.genre`, `author.summary`, `author.more`, `author.credits`, `author.wikipedia`, `author.photoCredit`, `author.photoFile`, `author.photoLicence`, `author.series`, `author.standalone`, `author.other`, `author.seriesParent`, `author.groupTitle`, `author.groupCount`, `author.work`, `author.workLink`, `author.workTitle`, `author.workMeta`, `author.workStatus`, `author.workWant`, `author.workFind`, `author.showAll`, `author.noWorks`, `author.loading`, `author.missing`, `author.retry`, `book.authors`, `book.author`, `library.entryAuthor`
Flow: no flow; data layer `web/tests/enrich.test.ts`, the rules `web/tests/enrich-present.test.ts`, database `supabase/tests/enrichment_test.sql`.
Native: a pushed view; the same RPCs; `utils/enrich.ts` ported as is.

### Book page sections, and More from the author  (web: `components/book/Section.vue` (the shell every section below About uses), `components/book/MoreFromAuthor.vue` under About in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`, `components/author/WorkRow.vue`, `moreFromAuthor`/`isCurrentWork` in `utils/enrich.ts`, `stores/authors.ts` (`load`, `page`, `ofBook`); no database change: it reads `author_page` like the author page, and the device's twelve kept pages)
Purpose (owner: "fill the book detail page more"): below About the Book page has sections of more to explore; the first is the author's other books. Every section is a `BookSection` (docs/MOTION.md, Reveal): **it starts only when it nears the viewport** (one IntersectionObserver on its root, a screen ahead, then gone; without the API, at once), reserves nothing and has no placeholders, and **opens whole (heading and content) when it has something to show**; with nothing — no data, offline and never seen, an error — it is not in the page at all: never an empty heading, never an error state. Reduce Motion: a short fade. A Book page glanced at and left asks for nothing below About.
Layout (More from the author, `book.authorMore`): the eyebrow heading `book.authorMore`; her name under it, a link to her page with a chevron; up to three of her other works as rows exactly as on her page (`AuthorWorkRow`: cover, title, year, her status ✓ with stars / Reading / DNF / Want to read, or **+ Want to read** / **Find**); then **Show all n** (`author.showAll`, n = every work her page lists) to her page, only where she has more than the three shown. Only the books: no portrait, intro, dates or credits (owner decision; they are her page's, so the Wikipedia and photo credits do not appear here either). **Covers** (A9): the rows' covers are `eager` and asked for (`coversToPrefetch`, `new Image()`) the moment her page arrives, at the size the rows ask for, so none pops in a second late behind the section's reveal; each row has OpenLibrary's cover of its edition by ISBN as a fallback (`workCoverImages`), and a work she has sits on her Book's thumbhash and colours while it loads (a work card carries none of its own; any other sits on the quiet fill).
Which works: her page's works (series in order, then novels, then other), this Book's own work left out (her entry of it, its edition's key or ISBN, or the same title), each work once; works that open a Book first (no edition to open: last), the first three. Which author: the **first linked author** in credit order (`rowAuthorKey`, as a Library row's name); the others are links on the author line, one tap away. No section for: a Book with no linked author (a search result not in the Catalogue, or nobody has enriched it yet), an author with no other work known, or while her page has not come (offline and never opened on this device, an error).
States: nothing (not near, loading, no author, no other works, offline without a copy, error: nothing in the page) · content (from the device's copy at once when she has been seen, refreshed behind it by `authors.load`: the rows' statuses follow her Library as everywhere)
Actions → result:
- Her name → her page (`/author/<key>`, the author push).
- A work → its Book's page, the cover flying like every row (`UiPressLink`; `/book/<her Book>`, else the edition by ISBN or Open Library key).
- **+ Want to read** / **Find** → as on her page (Add sheet / search); offline they say Offline.
- **Show all n** → her page.
Edge cases: the page is cached like the author pages (the device's copy of the last twelve, `libellus.enrich`), so the section shows offline from what she has seen; `authors.load` asks the sources once a session for a stale page, as her page does. A change of edition keeps the page, the section follows the new Book. Adding a new section later (Similar books): `<BookSection v-model:near :show :title :testid>`, start its data on `near`, `show` only with something to show, as this one does.

### Series line, series sheet and correction  (web: `components/series/{Line,Sheet,EditSheet}.vue` under the author line in `app/pages/book/[key].vue`, the Series row in `components/book/OptionsSheet.vue`, `components/author/WorkRow.vue`, `stores/series.ts`, `data/enrich/series.ts`, `seriesLine`/`parsePosition` in `utils/enrich.ts`; database: `book_series_info`, `series_works`, `set_entry_series`, `reset_entry_series`)
Purpose (#167): where a Book stands in its series, the series with her statuses, and her own word on it.
Layout: on the Book page, right under the author: "Book 4 of 8 · City Watch" (`series.bookOf` · name; `series.book` for a place between two or past the count, the name alone where the series gives none) with a chevron, from the most specific series. It opens the **series sheet** (title: the series' name): "Part of Discworld · 11 books" in mono, the works as on the author page, the Book it was opened from marked `series.thisBook` and scrolled into view, then for her Book `series.mine` (when she set it) and **Correct series**. The **edit sheet** (`series.editTitle`, Save at the top right): the rows Series (name) and Book (place, `2 or 2.5`), the hint, then **Not in a series** and, once she has corrected it, **Use the suggested series**. The Book's options carry the row **Series** for a Book that shows none.
States: no series (no line) · the line (at once from the device's copy; the first time it opens its room like Goodreads' line, once the cover's flight has landed) · sheet · edit · invalid place (`series.error.position`) · refused (`series.error.<code>`) · offline (Save, Not in a series and Use the suggested series disabled; Save reads Offline)
Actions → result:
- Tap the line → the sheet (`series_works` refreshes it). A work → its Book's page (the sheet goes with the page).
- Save → `set_entry_series(entry, series, null, place)` when the name is the series' own, else by name (`…, null, name, place`: the Catalogue's series of that name, else a new one that is hers alone); the line shows it at once and after a reload.
- Not in a series → `set_entry_series(entry, null, null, null)`: the line goes. Use the suggested series → `reset_entry_series(entry)`.
Edge cases: places may have two decimals (0.5 a prequel, 2.5 a novella), 0 ≤ place < 10000; a comma is a decimal point. "of n" counts the whole-numbered places. Her correction is per Library entry and follows Change edition. A series she named is visible to her only. Corrections need a connection.
