### Delete account  (web: the last row in `components/profile/Account.vue`, `deleteAccount` in `data/auth.ts`, `deleteAccount` / `accountDeleted` in `stores/session.ts`, the note in `pages/sign-in.vue`; server: `supabase/migrations/20261005101000_delete_my_account.sql`)
Purpose: a member deletes her account and everything of hers from inside the app (Google Play's in-app deletion rule; issue #101). Irreversible.
Layout: the **Delete account** row (`profile.account.delete`, trash icon, danger colour) at the end of the Profile's account rows, under Sign out. Its Confirm (`UiConfirm`, testid `deleteAccount`): the title `profile.deleteAccount.title`, the text `profile.deleteAccount.text` naming what goes (Library, reading sessions, ratings, reviews, collections, name and photo, who you follow and who follows you, sign-in) and that it cannot be undone, the danger button `profile.deleteAccount.action`, Cancel.
States: row enabled · offline (the row is disabled and says "Offline", `common.offline`: deleting is online-only) · Confirm · busy (buttons disabled) · failed (`profile.deleteAccount.failed` in the Confirm; nothing was removed, the same button tries again) · waiting changes (the Confirm's text gets `profile.deleteAccount.unsynced` appended with their number).
Actions → result:
- Tap the row → the Confirm rises. Cancel, the scrim, Escape or Back close it; nothing happens.
- Confirm → her photo first (#156: `set_avatar(null)`, then her folder in `avatars` emptied through the Storage API; `delete_my_account` refuses with `photo_remains` while a file is left), then **one call**, the RPC `delete_my_account()` (security definer; no argument, it deletes `auth.uid()` only): deletes the member's `auth.users` row, and everything she owns goes with it by `on delete cascade` (account, Library entries → reads → progress days, collection memberships, collections, her manual Books, her reader places and highlights, her follows, requests and blocks, her follow link and its views, her activity (the feed's entries), her privacy settings, her reading page and the Books she shared (#171), the record of synced writes, Supabase's sessions and identities). What stays: shared Catalogue Books, the Goodreads cache, the invite codes (the code she came in on stays spent). Then the session is dropped on the device (`signOut({ scope: 'local' })`), and the device forgets her as Sign out does: every `libellus.` key (the Library's copy, the pending address) and the IndexedDB database with the outbox — **writes still waiting to sync are discarded, never sent**. The theme stays (it is the device's). The app goes to **Sign in**, which shows `signIn.accountDeleted` once (`signIn.deleted`); it is gone when she asks for a code or reloads.
Edge cases: offline the repository refuses before anything is sent (`offline`); a failed call (`unknown`) removes nothing, on the server or the device. The same address has no account any more: Sign in sends it to Sign up, and it needs a **new invite** (the spent one stays spent). A member who can no longer sign in asks by email (the privacy page).
Copy keys: `profile.account.delete`, `profile.deleteAccount.title|text|unsynced|action|failed`, `signIn.accountDeleted`, `common.offline`, `common.cancel`
IDs: `profile.delete`, `deleteAccount`, `deleteAccount.title`, `deleteAccount.text`, `deleteAccount.error`, `deleteAccount.confirm`, `deleteAccount.cancel`, `deleteAccount.scrim`, `signIn.deleted`
Flow: `web/e2e/delete-account.spec.ts` (a fresh member signs up with an invite, adds a manual book, the Profile's last row opens the Confirm, Cancel changes nothing, Delete → Sign in with the note, nothing of her in the database or on the device, a reload without the note, the address needs a new invite and the spent one is refused) and `web/e2e/delete-account-offline.spec.ts` (offline the row is disabled and says Offline; with a change waiting the Confirm names it and deleting discards it); the repository in `web/tests/delete-account.test.ts`; the database in `supabase/tests/delete_my_account_test.sql`.
- [x] Web  - [ ] iOS  - [ ] Android

### Reading page and Book cards  (web: `app/pages/r/[token]/index.vue`, `app/pages/r/[token]/book/[book].vue`, `components/reading/{CoverRow,FinishedList,Footer}.vue`, `stores/readingPage.ts`; her side `components/profile/Sharing.vue` in `app/pages/profile/index.vue`, `components/sharing/Sheet.vue`, `components/sharing/BookSheet.vue` behind Share in `components/book/OptionsSheet.vue`, `stores/sharing.ts`, `utils/shareLink.ts`; both `data/readingPage.ts`, `utils/publicPath.ts` in `middleware/auth.global.ts`, `navigateFallbackDenylist` in `nuxt.config.ts`; hosting `web/functions/r/[[path]].js` (link previews, Open Graph images) and `supabase/functions/reading-page-og`; database: `supabase/migrations/20261011030000_reading_pages.sql` — `reading_pages`, `reading_page_books`, `set_reading_page`, `renew_reading_page_link`, `set_reading_page_sections`, `share_book_card`, `unshare_book_card`, `public_reading_page`, `public_book_card`)
Purpose (#171, part of #166): send what she reads to friends, who need no account and no follow: one public page per member, off by default, at an unguessable link she can revoke, and a card per Book. A share, not a follow: the followers she has in the app see what she chose under Friends → Privacy.
Layout — **Profile → Share**: a section **Share** before Account with one row **Reading page** (share icon) reading On or Off, opening the sheet `sharing.row`: what it is (`sharing.about`), the switch **Reading page**; on, **Your link** in mono with **Share link** (the platform's share sheet; the clipboard where there is none), **Copy link**, **Open page**, a polite line (`sharing.copied`); **On the page**: one switch per section (Currently reading, This year, Favourites, Recently finished, Your shelf) and how reviews get on it (`sharing.reviews`); **New link** (behind a Confirm `sharing.renewConfirm.*`); last what never shows (`sharing.never`). **Book → ⋯ → Share** (first row, only for a Book in her Library): the sheet `sharing.book.title` with the Book's line; with the page off `sharing.book.off` and **Turn on reading page**; on, **Include my review** (a switch, only when the Book's latest read has a review) and **Share** / **Copy link**. **Reading page** `/r/<token>` (no tab bar, no sign-in, the viewer's own light or dark, lit by the first cover it shows): the wordmark, the heading "<Name>'s reading" (`readingPage.titleNone` without a name), then the sections she left on, in order: **Currently reading** (up to 6, a row of covers with title and author), **<year> so far** (the Profile's four figures and its month row, as figures, not buttons), **Favourites** (her best Ratings, 4 stars and up, up to 12, covers with stars), **Recently finished** (her latest 12 finished Books: cover, title, author, stars, the day, and her review where she shared it), **<Name>'s shelf** (the owner's: Regal's 3D row from the published library file, as on her Profile; anyone else's: a row of the covers she finished, up to 60). Every Book opens its card, the tapped cover flying into the card's hero and, on Back, into its row (the app's cover flight, docs/MOTION.md). At the end a quiet footer: `readingPage.footer.tagline`, the **waitlist form** (see Waitlist, below) and, for whoever already has a code, **Have an invite code? Sign up**. The rows that scroll sideways (Currently reading, Favourites, the shelf) rest at their start inside the page's side padding and run edge to edge (`scroll-padding` equal to their padding). **Book card** `/r/<token>/book/<id>` (its hero, `bookCard.hero`, is where the cover lands): a way back to her page (the browser's Back when the card was opened from it, so the cover flies back), the cover large in its light, title, authors, where she is with it (`readingPage.card.reading|want|finished`), "<Name>'s rating" with the stars, "<Name>'s review" when she shared it, the footer.
Data: only through `public_reading_page(token)` and `public_book_card(token, book)` (security definer, granted to `anon`): the sections she turned on and nothing else — Books (title, authors, year, cover), her first name, Ratings, finish days, this year's counts, the reviews she opted into. Never her address, id, notes, highlights, progress, abandon reasons, collections or an unshared review. A card exists for a Book she shared or one her page shows; any other Book, and every dead token, is null ("not found", never "forbidden"). The owner is whoever `private.shelf_publish.owner_id` names (whose Library the published shelf is, #110).
States: her sheet — loading · off · on · offline (switches and New link disabled, `common.offline`) · a write refused (`sharing.error.*`) · could not load (`sharing.loadError`, Try again). Book sheet — off · on · with/without a review. Page and card — loading (`readingPage.loading`) · content · nothing to show (`readingPage.nothing`) · missing (`readingPage.missing.*`) · offline (`readingPage.offline`) · error (`readingPage.error`, Try again).
Actions → result:
- Switch on → `set_reading_page(true)`: a 22-character token (16 random bytes, base64url); the link shows. Off → `set_reading_page(false)`: the token is forgotten, every address handed out is dead; on again → a new link. New link → `renew_reading_page_link()`, the old link dies at once.
- A section switch → `set_reading_page_sections({ <section>: bool })`, written at once.
- Book → Share / Copy link → `share_book_card(book, review)` and, in the same tap (Safari keeps the share sheet for a fresh tap only), the link handed to the share sheet or the clipboard. Include my review → the review shows on the card and in Recently finished.
- A visitor taps a Book → its card (the cover flies into the hero); the card's back link → the page; Join the waitlist → "You're on the list"; Have an invite code? Sign up → Sign up.
Edge cases: the page and its cards are open to anyone, signed in or not (the auth middleware lets `/r/…` through); the service worker leaves them to the network, so the Pages Function answers a dead link with a 404 and puts the link preview's tags (`og:*`, `twitter:card`, `robots: noindex`) and its Open Graph image (rendered on demand, cached at Cloudflare's edge, docs/HOSTING.md) into the shell. A Book removed from her Library loses its card; an edition change moves it. Deleting her account deletes the page and the shared Books.
Copy keys: `sharing.*`, `readingPage.*`, `bookOptions.share`
IDs: `profile.share`, `profile.readingPage`, `profile.readingPageValue`, `sharing`, `sharing.on`, `sharing.link`, `sharing.share`, `sharing.copy`, `sharing.open`, `sharing.outcome`, `sharing.section.<reading|year|favourites|finished|shelf>`, `sharing.renew`, `sharing.error`, `sharing.loadError`, `sharing.retry`, `sharingRenew`, `bookOptions.share`, `shareBook`, `shareBook.off`, `shareBook.turnOn`, `shareBook.review`, `shareBook.share`, `shareBook.copy`, `shareBook.outcome`, `readingPage`, `readingPage.title`, `readingPage.loading`, `readingPage.missing`, `readingPage.error`, `readingPage.retry`, `readingPage.reading`, `readingPage.readingRow`, `readingPage.year`, `readingPage.months`, `readingPage.favourites`, `readingPage.favouritesRow`, `readingPage.finishedSection`, `readingPage.finished`, `readingPage.finishedBook`, `readingPage.review`, `readingPage.shelf`, `readingPage.shelfRow`, `readingPage.shelfCovers`, `readingPage.empty`, `readingPage.footer`, `readingPage.signUp`, `bookCard`, `bookCard.hero`, `bookCard.page`, `bookCard.title`, `bookCard.authors`, `bookCard.status`, `bookCard.rating`, `bookCard.review`, `bookCard.missing`
Flow: `web/e2e/reading-page.spec.ts` (signed out: her sections, a card, the invite, axe in the dark room · a new link and off leave the old address on "This page isn't here" · her side: Profile → Share on, a section off, a Book shared with her review, the waitlist form); Share with the page off, the cover flights and the owner's Regal row have no flow; data layer `web/tests/reading-page.test.ts`; the Pages Function `web/tests/reading-page-function.test.ts`; rules `supabase/tests/reading_pages_test.sql`.
Native: the same tables and functions; `data/readingPage.ts` ported 1:1; the page stays on the web (a native app shares the web address).
